===== apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/api/locatarioConfiguracoesApi.ts =====

// apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/api/locatarioConfiguracoesApi.ts

import { httpClient } from "../../../../core/api/httpClient";

export type BrandingLocatario = {
  locatarioId: string;
  configuracaoId: string;
  modoTema?: string | null;
  identidadeVisual?: string | null;
  corPrimaria?: string | null;
  corSecundaria?: string | null;
  corTerciaria?: string | null;
  urlLogo?: string | null;
  ativa?: boolean;
  atualizadoEm?: string | null;
};

export type AtualizarBrandingLocatarioInput = {
  nomeFantasia?: string | null;
  corPrimaria?: string | null;
  corSecundaria?: string | null;
  corFundo?: string | null;
  corTexto?: string | null;
  logoUrl?: string | null;
};

export type ModuloAssinaturaLocatario = {
  codigo: string;
  origem?: string | null;
};

export type ObterBrandingLocatarioResponse = { item: BrandingLocatario };
export type AtualizarBrandingLocatarioResponse = { item: BrandingLocatario };
export type ListarModulosAssinaturaLocatarioResponse = {
  locatarioId: string;
  modulos: ModuloAssinaturaLocatario[];
};
export type VincularModuloAssinaturaLocatarioInput = { codigo: string };
export type VincularModuloAssinaturaLocatarioResponse = {
  item: ModuloAssinaturaLocatario;
};
export type DesvincularModuloAssinaturaLocatarioResponse = {
  item: { codigo: string };
};

export const locatarioConfiguracoesApi = {
  obterBranding(locatarioId: string): Promise<ObterBrandingLocatarioResponse> {
    return httpClient.get<ObterBrandingLocatarioResponse>(
      `/plataforma/locatarios/${locatarioId}/configuracoes/ativa`,
    );
  },

  atualizarBranding(locatarioId: string, input: AtualizarBrandingLocatarioInput): Promise<AtualizarBrandingLocatarioResponse> {
    return httpClient.patch<
      AtualizarBrandingLocatarioResponse,
      AtualizarBrandingLocatarioInput
    >(`/plataforma/locatarios/${locatarioId}/branding`, input);
  },

  listarModulos(locatarioId: string): Promise<ListarModulosAssinaturaLocatarioResponse> {
    return httpClient.get<ListarModulosAssinaturaLocatarioResponse>(
      `/plataforma/locatarios/${locatarioId}/modulos`,
    );
  },

  vincularModulo(locatarioId: string, input: VincularModuloAssinaturaLocatarioInput): Promise<VincularModuloAssinaturaLocatarioResponse> {
    return httpClient.post<
      VincularModuloAssinaturaLocatarioResponse,
      VincularModuloAssinaturaLocatarioInput
    >(`/plataforma/locatarios/${locatarioId}/modulos`, input);
  },

  desvincularModulo(locatarioId: string, codigo: string): Promise<DesvincularModuloAssinaturaLocatarioResponse> {
    return httpClient.delete<DesvincularModuloAssinaturaLocatarioResponse>(
      `/plataforma/locatarios/${locatarioId}/modulos/${encodeURIComponent(codigo)}`,
    );
  },
};


===== apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/api/locatariosApi.ts =====

// apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/api/locatariosApi.ts

import { httpClient } from "../../../../core/api/httpClient";
import { AtualizarConfiguracaoLocatarioInput, CriarConfiguracaoLocatarioInput } from "../../../tenant-admin/locatario";
import type { AcaoLocatarioResponse, AtualizarLocatarioInput, AtualizarLocatarioResponse, CriarLocatarioInput, CriarLocatarioResponse, EncerrarLocatarioInput,
  ListarLocatariosResponse, ObterLocatarioResponse, ReativarLocatarioInput, SuspenderLocatarioInput } from "../tipos";

const BASE_PLATAFORMA = "/plataforma/locatarios";
const BASE = "/locatarios";
const BASE_ME = "/me/locatario";

export const locatariosApi = {
  listar(): Promise<ListarLocatariosResponse> {
    return httpClient.get<ListarLocatariosResponse>(BASE_PLATAFORMA);
  },

  obter(locatarioId: string): Promise<ObterLocatarioResponse> {
    return httpClient.get<ObterLocatarioResponse>(`${BASE_PLATAFORMA}/${locatarioId}`);
  },

  criar(input: CriarLocatarioInput): Promise<CriarLocatarioResponse> {
    return httpClient.post<CriarLocatarioResponse, CriarLocatarioInput>(
      BASE_PLATAFORMA,
      input,
    );
  },

  atualizar(locatarioId: string, input: AtualizarLocatarioInput): Promise<AtualizarLocatarioResponse> {
    return httpClient.patch<AtualizarLocatarioResponse, AtualizarLocatarioInput>(
      `${BASE_PLATAFORMA}/${locatarioId}`,
      input,
    );
  },

  suspender(locatarioId: string, input: SuspenderLocatarioInput = {}): Promise<AcaoLocatarioResponse> {
    return httpClient.post<AcaoLocatarioResponse, SuspenderLocatarioInput>(
      `${BASE_PLATAFORMA}/${locatarioId}/suspender`,
      input,
    );
  },

  reativar(locatarioId: string, input: ReativarLocatarioInput = {}): Promise<AcaoLocatarioResponse> {
    return httpClient.post<AcaoLocatarioResponse, ReativarLocatarioInput>(
      `${BASE_PLATAFORMA}/${locatarioId}/reativar`,
      input,
    );
  },

  encerrar(locatarioId: string, input: EncerrarLocatarioInput = {}): Promise<AcaoLocatarioResponse> {
    return httpClient.post<AcaoLocatarioResponse, EncerrarLocatarioInput>(
      `${BASE_PLATAFORMA}/${locatarioId}/encerrar`,
      input,
    );
  },

  // próprio locatário
  obterMeu(): Promise<ObterLocatarioResponse> {
    // o backend ignora o :id e usa ctx.locatarioId
    return httpClient.get(`${BASE}/me`);
  },

  // 👇 CONFIGURAÇÕES (NOVO CONTRATO)
  listarConfiguracoes() {
    return httpClient.get(`${BASE_ME}/configuracoes`);
  },

  obterConfiguracaoAtiva() {
    return httpClient.get(`${BASE_ME}/configuracoes/ativa`);
  },

  criarConfiguracao(input: CriarConfiguracaoLocatarioInput) {
    return httpClient.post(`${BASE_ME}/configuracoes`, input);
  },

  atualizarConfiguracao(configuracaoId: string, input: AtualizarConfiguracaoLocatarioInput) {
    return httpClient.patch(
      `${BASE_ME}/configuracoes/${configuracaoId}`,
      input,
    );
  },

  ativarConfiguracao(configuracaoId: string) {
    return httpClient.post(
      `${BASE_ME}/configuracoes/${configuracaoId}/ativar`,
    );
  },
};

===== apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/components/LocatarioBrandingForm.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/components/LocatarioBrandingForm.tsx

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import type { AtualizarBrandingLocatarioInput, BrandingLocatario } from "../api/locatarioConfiguracoesApi";

type Props = {
  branding: BrandingLocatario | null;
  isSaving: boolean;
  onSubmit: (input: AtualizarBrandingLocatarioInput) => void | Promise<void>;
};

export function LocatarioBrandingForm({ branding, isSaving, onSubmit }: Props) {
  const [nomeFantasia, setNomeFantasia] = useState("");
  const [logoUrl, setLogoUrl] = useState("");
  const [corPrimaria, setCorPrimaria] = useState("");
  const [corSecundaria, setCorSecundaria] = useState("");
  const [corFundo, setCorFundo] = useState("");
  const [corTexto, setCorTexto] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    setNomeFantasia(branding?.identidadeVisual ?? "");
    setLogoUrl(branding?.urlLogo ?? "");
    setCorPrimaria(branding?.corPrimaria ?? "");
    setCorSecundaria(branding?.corSecundaria ?? "");
    setCorFundo(branding?.corTerciaria ?? "");
  }, [branding]);

  async function handleSubmit(event: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();
    await onSubmit({
      nomeFantasia: nomeFantasia.trim() || null,
      logoUrl: logoUrl.trim() || null,
      corPrimaria: corPrimaria.trim() || null,
      corSecundaria: corSecundaria.trim() || null,
      corFundo: corFundo.trim() || null,
      corTexto: corTexto.trim() || null,
    });
  }

  return (
    <form className="grid gap-4 rounded-2xl border bg-white p-6" onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
      <div>
        <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">Branding</h2>
        <p className="text-sm text-slate-600">Atualize as informações visuais usadas pelo locatário.</p>
      </div>
      <input className="rounded-xl border px-3 py-2" placeholder="Nome fantasia" value={nomeFantasia} onChange={(event) => setNomeFantasia(event.target.value)} />
      <input className="rounded-xl border px-3 py-2" placeholder="URL do logo" value={logoUrl} onChange={(event) => setLogoUrl(event.target.value)} />
      <input className="rounded-xl border px-3 py-2" placeholder="Cor primária. Ex.: #2563eb" value={corPrimaria} onChange={(event) => setCorPrimaria(event.target.value)} />
      <input className="rounded-xl border px-3 py-2" placeholder="Cor secundária. Ex.: #1d4ed8" value={corSecundaria} onChange={(event) => setCorSecundaria(event.target.value)} />
      <input className="rounded-xl border px-3 py-2" placeholder="Cor de fundo. Ex.: #ffffff" value={corFundo} onChange={(event) => setCorFundo(event.target.value)} />
      <input className="rounded-xl border px-3 py-2" placeholder="Cor do texto. Ex.: #111827" value={corTexto} onChange={(event) => setCorTexto(event.target.value)} />
      <div className="flex justify-end">
        <button className="rounded-xl border px-4 py-2" type="submit" disabled={isSaving}>{isSaving ? "Salvando..." : "Salvar branding"}</button>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}


===== apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/components/LocatarioBrandingResumo.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/components/LocatarioBrandingResumo.tsx

import type { BrandingLocatario } from "../api/locatarioConfiguracoesApi";

type Props = { branding: BrandingLocatario | null };

export function LocatarioBrandingResumo({ branding }: Props) {
  if (!branding) {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-xl border bg-white p-4 text-sm text-slate-600">
        Nenhuma configuração de branding encontrada.
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="grid gap-2 rounded-2xl border bg-white p-6 text-sm">
      <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">Branding</h2>
      <div><strong>Nome fantasia:</strong> {branding.identidadeVisual ?? "—"}</div>
      <div><strong>Logo:</strong> {branding.urlLogo ?? "—"}</div>
      <div><strong>Cor primária:</strong> {branding.corPrimaria ?? "—"}</div>
      <div><strong>Cor secundária:</strong> {branding.corSecundaria ?? "—"}</div>
      <div><strong>Cor terciária:</strong> {branding.corTerciaria ?? "—"}</div>
      <div><strong>Modo de tema:</strong> {branding.modoTema ?? "—"}</div>
      <div><strong>Atualizado em:</strong> {branding.atualizadoEm ?? "—"}</div>
    </div>
  );
}


===== apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/components/LocatarioForm.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/components/LocatarioForm.tsx

import { useMemo, useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { SuperAdminButton, SuperAdminCard } from "../../shared";
import type { AtualizarLocatarioInput, CriarLocatarioInput, Locatario, ModoTema } from "../tipos";
import "../../shared/superAdminPages.css";

type Props = {
  mode: "create" | "edit";
  initialData?: Locatario | null;
  isSaving?: boolean;
  erro?: string | null;
  onSubmit: (
    input: CriarLocatarioInput | AtualizarLocatarioInput,
  ) => void | Promise<void>;
};

function normalizeOptionalText(value: string): string | null {
  const trimmed = value.trim();
  return trimmed ? trimmed : null;
}

export function LocatarioForm({
  mode,
  initialData,
  isSaving,
  erro,
  onSubmit,
}: Props) {
  const [nome, setNome] = useState(initialData?.nome ?? "");
  const [slug, setSlug] = useState(initialData?.slug ?? "");
  const [email, setEmail] = useState(initialData?.email ?? "");
  const [documento, setDocumento] = useState(initialData?.documento ?? "");
  const [fusoHorario, setFusoHorario] = useState(
    initialData?.fusoHorario ?? "America/Sao_Paulo",
  );

  const [identidadeVisual, setIdentidadeVisual] = useState(
    initialData?.nome ?? "",
  );
  const [modoTema, setModoTema] = useState<ModoTema>("CLARO");
  const [corPrimaria, setCorPrimaria] = useState("#2563eb");
  const [corSecundaria, setCorSecundaria] = useState("#1d4ed8");
  const [corTerciaria, setCorTerciaria] = useState("#111827");
  const [urlLogo, setUrlLogo] = useState("");

  const titulo = useMemo(
    () => (mode === "create" ? "Novo locatário" : "Editar locatário"),
    [mode],
  );

  const descricao = useMemo(
    () =>
      mode === "create"
        ? "Cadastre uma nova empresa/tenant da plataforma."
        : "Atualize os dados principais do locatário.",
    [mode],
  );

  async function handleSubmit(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();

    if (mode === "create") {
      await onSubmit({
        nome: nome.trim(),
        slug: slug.trim(),
        email: email.trim(),
        documento: normalizeOptionalText(documento),
        fusoHorario: fusoHorario.trim() || "America/Sao_Paulo",
        configuracaoInicial: {
          modoTema,
          identidadeVisual: identidadeVisual.trim() || nome.trim(),
          corPrimaria: normalizeOptionalText(corPrimaria),
          corSecundaria: normalizeOptionalText(corSecundaria),
          corTerciaria: normalizeOptionalText(corTerciaria),
          urlLogo: normalizeOptionalText(urlLogo),
          ativa: true,
        },
      });

      return;
    }

    await onSubmit({
      nome: nome.trim(),
      email: email.trim(),
      documento: normalizeOptionalText(documento),
      fusoHorario: fusoHorario.trim() || "America/Sao_Paulo",
    });
  }

  return (
    <form className="ck-superadmin-page" onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
      <header className="ck-superadmin-page__header">
        <div>
          <p className="ck-superadmin-page__kicker">Locatários</p>
          <h1>{titulo}</h1>
          <p>{descricao}</p>
        </div>
      </header>

      {erro ? (
        <SuperAdminCard title="Erro ao salvar">
          {erro}
        </SuperAdminCard>
      ) : null}

      <SuperAdminCard
        title="Dados principais"
        description="Informações de identificação e acesso do locatário."
      >
        <div className="ck-superadmin-form-grid">
          <label className="ck-superadmin-field ck-superadmin-field--wide">
            <span>Nome</span>
            <input
              value={nome}
              onChange={(event) => setNome(event.target.value)}
              placeholder="Ex.: Loja Modelo"
              required
            />
          </label>

          <label className="ck-superadmin-field">
            <span>Slug</span>
            <input
              value={slug}
              onChange={(event) => setSlug(event.target.value)}
              placeholder="Ex.: loja"
              disabled={mode === "edit"}
              required={mode === "create"}
            />
          </label>

          <label className="ck-superadmin-field">
            <span>E-mail administrativo</span>
            <input
              type="email"
              value={email}
              onChange={(event) => setEmail(event.target.value)}
              placeholder="admin@empresa.com"
              required
            />
          </label>

          <label className="ck-superadmin-field">
            <span>Documento</span>
            <input
              value={documento}
              onChange={(event) => setDocumento(event.target.value)}
              placeholder="CNPJ, CPF ou identificador"
            />
          </label>

          <label className="ck-superadmin-field">
            <span>Fuso horário</span>
            <input
              value={fusoHorario}
              onChange={(event) => setFusoHorario(event.target.value)}
              placeholder="America/Sao_Paulo"
            />
          </label>
        </div>
      </SuperAdminCard>

      {mode === "create" ? (
        <SuperAdminCard
          title="Configuração inicial"
          description="Identidade visual inicial criada junto com o locatário."
        >
          <div className="ck-superadmin-form-grid">
            <label className="ck-superadmin-field ck-superadmin-field--wide">
              <span>Identidade visual</span>
              <input
                value={identidadeVisual}
                onChange={(event) => setIdentidadeVisual(event.target.value)}
                placeholder="Ex.: Loja Modelo"
              />
            </label>

            <label className="ck-superadmin-field">
              <span>Modo de tema</span>
              <select
                value={modoTema}
                onChange={(event) => setModoTema(event.target.value as ModoTema)}
              >
                <option value="CLARO">Claro</option>
                <option value="ESCURO">Escuro</option>
                <option value="SISTEMA">Sistema</option>
              </select>
            </label>

            <label className="ck-superadmin-field">
              <span>Cor primária</span>
              <input
                value={corPrimaria}
                onChange={(event) => setCorPrimaria(event.target.value)}
                placeholder="#2563eb"
              />
            </label>

            <label className="ck-superadmin-field">
              <span>Cor secundária</span>
              <input
                value={corSecundaria}
                onChange={(event) => setCorSecundaria(event.target.value)}
                placeholder="#1d4ed8"
              />
            </label>

            <label className="ck-superadmin-field">
              <span>Cor terciária/texto</span>
              <input
                value={corTerciaria}
                onChange={(event) => setCorTerciaria(event.target.value)}
                placeholder="#111827"
              />
            </label>

            <label className="ck-superadmin-field ck-superadmin-field--wide">
              <span>URL do logo</span>
              <input
                value={urlLogo}
                onChange={(event) => setUrlLogo(event.target.value)}
                placeholder="https://..."
              />
            </label>
          </div>
        </SuperAdminCard>
      ) : null}

      <div className="ck-superadmin-page__actions">
        <SuperAdminButton type="submit" variant="primary" disabled={Boolean(isSaving)}>
          {isSaving ? "Salvando..." : "Salvar locatário"}
        </SuperAdminButton>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}


===== apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/components/LocatarioModulosForm.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/components/LocatarioModulosForm.tsx

import { useState } from "react";
import type { ModuloAssinaturaLocatario } from "../api/locatarioConfiguracoesApi";

type Props = {
  modulos: ModuloAssinaturaLocatario[];
  isRunning: boolean;
  onVincular: (codigo: string) => void | Promise<void>;
  onDesvincular: (codigo: string) => void | Promise<void>;
};

const MODULOS_SUGERIDOS = ["ASSINATURAS", "USUARIOS", "VENDAS", "COMPRAS", "ESTOQUE", "FINANCEIRO", "ORDENS_SERVICO"];

export function LocatarioModulosForm({ modulos, isRunning, onVincular, onDesvincular }: Props) {
  const [codigo, setCodigo] = useState("");
  const modulosAtuais = new Set(modulos.map((modulo) => modulo.codigo));
  const disponiveis = MODULOS_SUGERIDOS.filter((modulo) => !modulosAtuais.has(modulo));

  async function handleSubmit(event: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();
    await onVincular(codigo);
    setCodigo("");
  }

  return (
    <div className="grid gap-4 rounded-2xl border bg-white p-6">
      <div>
        <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">Módulos da assinatura</h2>
        <p className="text-sm text-slate-600">Acrescente ou remova módulos disponíveis para o locatário.</p>
      </div>

      <form className="flex flex-wrap gap-3" onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
        <input className="rounded-xl border px-3 py-2" placeholder="Código do módulo" value={codigo} onChange={(event) => setCodigo(event.target.value)} list="modulos-disponiveis" />
        <datalist id="modulos-disponiveis">{disponiveis.map((modulo) => <option key={modulo} value={modulo} />)}</datalist>
        <button className="rounded-xl border px-4 py-2" type="submit" disabled={isRunning}>Adicionar módulo</button>
      </form>

      {modulos.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="rounded-xl border p-4 text-sm text-slate-600">Nenhum módulo vinculado.</div>
      ) : (
        <div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-2xl border">
          <table className="min-w-full border-collapse text-sm">
            <thead>
              <tr className="border-b bg-slate-50 text-left"><th className="px-4 py-3">Código</th><th className="px-4 py-3">Origem</th><th className="px-4 py-3">Ações</th></tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {modulos.map((modulo) => (
                <tr key={modulo.codigo} className="border-b last:border-b-0">
                  <td className="px-4 py-3">{modulo.codigo}</td><td className="px-4 py-3">{modulo.origem ?? "—"}</td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3"><button className="text-red-600 underline disabled:opacity-50" type="button" disabled={isRunning} onClick={() => void onDesvincular(modulo.codigo)}>Remover</button></td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      )}

      {disponiveis.length > 0 ? <div className="text-sm text-slate-600"><strong>Sugestões disponíveis:</strong> {disponiveis.join(", ")}</div> : null}
    </div>
  );
}

              <td className="px-4 py-3">{modulo.origem ?? "—"}</td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>

    </div>
  );

===== apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/components/UsuariosLocatarioTable.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/components/UsuariosLocatarioTable.tsx

import type { UsuarioLocatario } from "../api/usuariosLocatarioApi";

type Props = {
  itens: UsuarioLocatario[];
  onEditar?: (usuarioId: string) => void;
  onBloquear?: (usuarioId: string) => void;
  onReativar?: (usuarioId: string) => void;
  onResetarSenha?: (usuarioId: string) => void;
};

export function UsuariosLocatarioTable({
  itens,
  onEditar,
  onBloquear,
  onReativar,
  onResetarSenha,
}: Props) {
  if (itens.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-xl border bg-white p-4 text-sm text-slate-600">
        Nenhum usuário cadastrado para este locatário.
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-2xl border bg-white">
      <table className="min-w-full border-collapse text-sm">
        <thead>
          <tr className="border-b bg-slate-50 text-left">
            <th className="px-4 py-3">Nome</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-3">E-mail</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-3">Perfil</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-3">Status</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-3">ID</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-3">Ações</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>

        <tbody>
          {itens.map((usuario) => {
            const status = usuario.status?.trim().toUpperCase();
            const ativo = status === "ATIVO";
            const bloqueado =
              status === "BLOQUEADO" ||
              status === "INATIVO" ||
              status === "SUSPENSO";

            return (
              <tr
                key={usuario.usuarioId}
                className="border-b last:border-b-0"
              >
                <td className="px-4 py-3">{usuario.nome}</td>
                <td className="px-4 py-3">{usuario.email}</td>
                <td className="px-4 py-3">{usuario.perfil ?? "—"}</td>
                <td className="px-4 py-3">{usuario.status ?? "—"}</td>
                <td className="px-4 py-3">{usuario.usuarioId}</td>
                <td className="px-4 py-3">
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
                    <button
                      className="text-blue-600 underline disabled:opacity-50"
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => onEditar?.(usuario.usuarioId)}
                    >
                      Editar
                    </button>

                    {ativo ? (
                      <button
                        className="text-red-600 underline disabled:opacity-50"
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => onBloquear?.(usuario.usuarioId)}
                      >
                        Bloquear
                      </button>
                    ) : null}

                    {bloqueado ? (
                      <button
                        className="text-green-700 underline disabled:opacity-50"
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => onReativar?.(usuario.usuarioId)}
                      >
                        Reativar
                      </button>
                    ) : null}

                    <button
                      className="text-blue-600 underline disabled:opacity-50"
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => onResetarSenha?.(usuario.usuarioId)}
                    >
                      Resetar senha
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </td>
              </tr>
            );
          })}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
}

===== apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/hooks/useLocatarioActions.ts =====

// apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/hooks/useLocatarioActions.ts

import { useState } from "react";
import { mapError } from "../../../../core/errors/mapError";
import { locatariosApi } from "../api/locatariosApi";

type ActionName = "suspender" | "reativar" | "encerrar";

export function useLocatarioActions(onSuccess?: () => void | Promise<void>) {
  const [isRunning, setIsRunning] = useState<ActionName | null>(null);
  const [erro, setErro] = useState<string | null>(null);

  async function suspender(locatarioId: string, motivoSuspensao?: string) {
    setIsRunning("suspender");
    setErro(null);

    try {
      await locatariosApi.suspender(locatarioId, { motivoSuspensao });
      await onSuccess?.();
      return true;
    } catch (error) {
      setErro(mapError(error).message);
      return false;
    } finally {
      setIsRunning(null);
    }
  }

  async function reativar(locatarioId: string, motivoReativacao?: string) {
    setIsRunning("reativar");
    setErro(null);

    try {
      await locatariosApi.reativar(locatarioId, { motivoReativacao });
      await onSuccess?.();
      return true;
    } catch (error) {
      setErro(mapError(error).message);
      return false;
    } finally {
      setIsRunning(null);
    }
  }

  async function encerrar(locatarioId: string, motivoExclusao?: string) {
    setIsRunning("encerrar");
    setErro(null);

    try {
      await locatariosApi.encerrar(locatarioId, { motivoExclusao });
      await onSuccess?.();
      return true;
    } catch (error) {
      setErro(mapError(error).message);
      return false;
    } finally {
      setIsRunning(null);
    }
  }

  return {
    erro,
    isRunning,
    suspender,
    reativar,
    encerrar,
  };
}

===== apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/hooks/useLocatarioConfiguracoesActions.ts =====

// apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/hooks/useLocatarioConfiguracoesActions.ts

import { useState } from "react";
import { mapError } from "../../../../core/errors/mapError";
import {
  locatarioConfiguracoesApi,
  type AtualizarBrandingLocatarioInput,
} from "../api/locatarioConfiguracoesApi";

type RunningAction = "branding" | "vincular-modulo" | "desvincular-modulo";

type Params = {
  locatarioId?: string;
  onSuccess?: () => void | Promise<void>;
};

export function useLocatarioConfiguracoesActions({ locatarioId, onSuccess }: Params) {
  const [isRunning, setIsRunning] = useState<RunningAction | null>(null);
  const [erro, setErro] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [sucesso, setSucesso] = useState<string | null>(null);

  async function finalizar(mensagem: string) {
    setSucesso(mensagem);
    await onSuccess?.();
  }

  async function atualizarBranding(input: AtualizarBrandingLocatarioInput) {
    if (!locatarioId?.trim()) {
      setErro("ID do locatário inválido.");
      return false;
    }

    setIsRunning("branding");
    setErro(null);
    setSucesso(null);

    try {
      await locatarioConfiguracoesApi.atualizarBranding(locatarioId, input);
      await finalizar("Branding atualizado com sucesso.");
      return true;
    } catch (error) {
      setErro(mapError(error).message);
      return false;
    } finally {
      setIsRunning(null);
    }
  }

  async function vincularModulo(codigo: string) {
    if (!locatarioId?.trim()) {
      setErro("ID do locatário inválido.");
      return false;
    }

    const codigoNormalizado = codigo.trim().toUpperCase();
    if (!codigoNormalizado) {
      setErro("Informe o código do módulo.");
      return false;
    }

    setIsRunning("vincular-modulo");
    setErro(null);
    setSucesso(null);

    try {
      await locatarioConfiguracoesApi.vincularModulo(locatarioId, {
        codigo: codigoNormalizado,
      });
      await finalizar("Módulo vinculado com sucesso.");
      return true;
    } catch (error) {
      setErro(mapError(error).message);
      return false;
    } finally {
      setIsRunning(null);
    }
  }

  async function desvincularModulo(codigo: string) {
    if (!locatarioId?.trim()) {
      setErro("ID do locatário inválido.");
      return false;
    }

    const codigoNormalizado = codigo.trim().toUpperCase();
    if (!codigoNormalizado) {
      setErro("Informe o código do módulo.");
      return false;
    }

    const confirmado = window.confirm(`Remover o módulo ${codigoNormalizado} deste locatário?`);
    if (!confirmado) return false;

    setIsRunning("desvincular-modulo");
    setErro(null);
    setSucesso(null);

    try {
      await locatarioConfiguracoesApi.desvincularModulo(locatarioId, codigoNormalizado);
      await finalizar("Módulo removido com sucesso.");
      return true;
    } catch (error) {
      setErro(mapError(error).message);
      return false;
    } finally {
      setIsRunning(null);
    }
  }

  return { atualizarBranding, vincularModulo, desvincularModulo, isRunning, erro, sucesso };
}


===== apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/hooks/useLocatarioConfiguracoes.ts =====

// apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/hooks/useLocatarioConfiguracoes.ts

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { mapError } from "../../../../core/errors/mapError";
import {
  locatarioConfiguracoesApi,
  type BrandingLocatario,
  type ModuloAssinaturaLocatario,
} from "../api/locatarioConfiguracoesApi";

export function useLocatarioConfiguracoes(locatarioId?: string) {
  const [branding, setBranding] = useState<BrandingLocatario | null>(null);
  const [modulos, setModulos] = useState<ModuloAssinaturaLocatario[]>([]);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [erro, setErro] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const carregar = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!locatarioId?.trim()) {
      setErro("ID do locatário inválido.");
      setIsLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    setIsLoading(true);
    setErro(null);

    try {
      const [brandingResponse, modulosResponse] = await Promise.all([
        locatarioConfiguracoesApi.obterBranding(locatarioId),
        locatarioConfiguracoesApi.listarModulos(locatarioId),
      ]);

      setBranding(brandingResponse.item);
      setModulos(modulosResponse.modulos);
    } catch (error) {
      setErro(mapError(error).message);
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  }, [locatarioId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    void carregar();
  }, [carregar]);

  return { branding, modulos, isLoading, erro, recarregar: carregar };
}


===== apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/hooks/useLocatarios.ts =====

// apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/hooks/useLocatarios.ts

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { mapError } from "../../../../core/errors/mapError";
import { locatariosApi } from "../api/locatariosApi";
import type { Locatario } from "../tipos";

export function useLocatarios() {
  const [itens, setItens] = useState<Locatario[]>([]);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [erro, setErro] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const carregar = useCallback(async () => {
    setIsLoading(true);
    setErro(null);

    try {
      const response = await locatariosApi.listar();
      setItens(response.itens);
    } catch (error) {
      setErro(mapError(error).message);
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    void carregar();
  }, [carregar]);

  return {
    itens,
    isLoading,
    erro,
    recarregar: carregar,
  };
}


===== apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/hooks/useLocatario.ts =====

// apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/hooks/useLocatario.ts

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { mapError } from "../../../../core/errors/mapError";
import { locatariosApi } from "../api/locatariosApi";
import type { Locatario } from "../tipos";

export function useLocatario(locatarioId?: string) {
  const [item, setItem] = useState<Locatario | null>(null);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [erro, setErro] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const carregar = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!locatarioId?.trim()) {
      setItem(null);
      setErro("ID do locatário inválido.");
      setIsLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    setIsLoading(true);
    setErro(null);

    try {
      const response = await locatariosApi.obter(locatarioId);
      setItem(response.item);
    } catch (error) {
      setErro(mapError(error).message);
      setItem(null);
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  }, [locatarioId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    void carregar();
  }, [carregar]);

  return {
    item,
    isLoading,
    erro,
    recarregar: carregar,
  };
}

===== apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/hooks/useSalvarLocatario.ts =====

// apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/hooks/useSalvarLocatario.ts

import { useState } from "react";
import { mapError } from "../../../../core/errors/mapError";
import { locatariosApi } from "../api/locatariosApi";
import type { AtualizarLocatarioInput, CriarLocatarioInput } from "../tipos";

type Params = {
  mode: "create" | "edit";
  locatarioId?: string;
  onSuccess?: (locatarioId?: string) => void | Promise<void>;
};

export function useSalvarLocatario({ mode, locatarioId, onSuccess, }: Params) {
  const [isSaving, setIsSaving] = useState(false);
  const [erro, setErro] = useState<string | null>(null);

  async function salvar(input: CriarLocatarioInput | AtualizarLocatarioInput) {
    setIsSaving(true);
    setErro(null);

    try {
      if (mode === "create") {
        const response = await locatariosApi.criar(input as CriarLocatarioInput);
        await onSuccess?.(response.item.locatarioId);
      } else {
        const id = locatarioId?.trim();

        if (!id) {
          setErro("ID do locatário inválido.");
          return false;
        }

        await locatariosApi.atualizar(id, input as AtualizarLocatarioInput);
        await onSuccess?.(id);
      }

      return true;
    } catch (error) {
      setErro(mapError(error).message);
      return false;
    } finally {
      setIsSaving(false);
    }
  }

  return {
    salvar,
    isSaving,
    erro,
  };
}

===== apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/hooks/useUsuariosLocatario.ts =====

// apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/hooks/useUsuariosLocatario.ts

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { mapError } from "../../../../core/errors/mapError";
import {
  usuariosLocatarioApi,
  type UsuarioLocatario,
} from "../api/usuariosLocatarioApi";

export function useUsuariosLocatario(locatarioId?: string) {
  const [itens, setItens] = useState<UsuarioLocatario[]>([]);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [erro, setErro] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const carregar = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!locatarioId?.trim()) {
      setErro("ID do locatário inválido.");
      setIsLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    setIsLoading(true);
    setErro(null);

    try {
      const response = await usuariosLocatarioApi.listar(locatarioId);
      setItens(response.itens);
    } catch (error) {
      setErro(mapError(error).message);
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  }, [locatarioId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    void carregar();
  }, [carregar]);

  return {
    itens,
    isLoading,
    erro,
    recarregar: carregar,
  };
}

===== apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/pages/EditarLocatarioPage.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/pages/EditarLocatarioPage.tsx

import { useNavigate, useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { SuperAdminButton, SuperAdminCard, SuperAdminPage } from "../../shared";
import { LocatarioForm } from "../components/LocatarioForm";
import { useLocatario } from "../hooks/useLocatario";
import { useSalvarLocatario } from "../hooks/useSalvarLocatario";
import type { AtualizarLocatarioInput, CriarLocatarioInput } from "../tipos";

export default function EditarLocatarioPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const params = useParams<{ locatarioId: string }>();
  const locatarioId = params.locatarioId?.trim();

  const { item, isLoading, erro: erroCarga } = useLocatario(locatarioId);

  const { salvar, isSaving, erro } = useSalvarLocatario({
    mode: "edit",
    locatarioId,
    onSuccess: (id) =>
      navigate(id ? `/plataforma/locatarios/${id}` : "/plataforma/locatarios"),
  });

  async function handleSubmit(input: CriarLocatarioInput | AtualizarLocatarioInput) {
    await salvar(input);
  }

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <SuperAdminPage
        kicker="Plataforma"
        title="Editar locatário"
        description="Carregando dados do locatário."
      >
        <SuperAdminCard>Carregando locatário...</SuperAdminCard>
      </SuperAdminPage>
    );
  }

  if (erroCarga) {
    return (
      <SuperAdminPage
        kicker="Plataforma"
        title="Editar locatário"
        description="Não foi possível carregar o locatário."
      >
        <SuperAdminCard title="Erro">{erroCarga}</SuperAdminCard>
      </SuperAdminPage>
    );
  }

  if (!item) {
    return (
      <SuperAdminPage
        kicker="Plataforma"
        title="Editar locatário"
        description="Registro não encontrado."
      >
        <SuperAdminCard>Locatário não encontrado.</SuperAdminCard>
      </SuperAdminPage>
    );
  }

  return (
    <SuperAdminPage
      kicker="Plataforma"
      title={`Editar ${item.nome}`}
      description="Atualize os dados principais do locatário."
      actions={
        <>
          <SuperAdminButton to={`/plataforma/locatarios/${item.locatarioId}`}>
            Voltar para detalhes
          </SuperAdminButton>

          <SuperAdminButton to="/plataforma/locatarios">
            Lista de locatários
          </SuperAdminButton>
        </>
      }
    >
      <LocatarioForm
        mode="edit"
        initialData={item}
        isSaving={isSaving}
        erro={erro}
        onSubmit={handleSubmit}
      />
    </SuperAdminPage>
  );
}


===== apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/pages/LocatarioDetailsPage.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/pages/LocatarioDetailsPage.tsx

import { useState } from "react";
import { useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { mapError } from "../../../../core/errors/mapError";
import { formatDateTime } from "../../../../shared/formatters";
import { SuperAdminButton, SuperAdminCard, SuperAdminDetailList, SuperAdminPage } from "../../shared";
import { locatariosApi } from "../api/locatariosApi";
import { useLocatario } from "../hooks/useLocatario";
import type { Locatario, StatusLocatario } from "../tipos";

function statusText(status: StatusLocatario): string {
  switch (status) {
    case "ATIVO":
      return "Ativo";
    case "SUSPENSO":
      return "Suspenso";
    case "ENCERRADO":
      return "Encerrado";
    case "INATIVO":
      return "Inativo";
    default:
      return status;
  }
}

export default function LocatarioDetailsPage() {
  const params = useParams<{ locatarioId: string }>();
  const locatarioId = params.locatarioId?.trim();

  const { item, isLoading, erro, recarregar } = useLocatario(locatarioId);
  const [erroAcao, setErroAcao] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isRunning, setIsRunning] = useState<
    "suspender" | "reativar" | "encerrar" | null
  >(null);

  async function executarAcao(acao: "suspender" | "reativar" | "encerrar") {
    if (!locatarioId) return;

    setIsRunning(acao);
    setErroAcao(null);

    try {
      if (acao === "suspender") {
        await locatariosApi.suspender(locatarioId, {
          motivoSuspensao: "Suspenso pela plataforma.",
        });
      }

      if (acao === "reativar") {
        await locatariosApi.reativar(locatarioId, {
          motivoReativacao: "Reativado pela plataforma.",
        });
      }

      if (acao === "encerrar") {
        await locatariosApi.encerrar(locatarioId, {
          motivoExclusao: "Encerrado pela plataforma.",
        });
      }

      await recarregar();
    } catch (error) {
      setErroAcao(mapError(error).message);
    } finally {
      setIsRunning(null);
    }
  }

  function renderDetails(locatario: Locatario) {
    return (
      <>
        <section className="ck-superadmin-detail-grid">
          <SuperAdminCard
            title="Identificação"
            description="Dados principais do locatário."
          >
            <SuperAdminDetailList
              items={[
                { label: "Nome", value: locatario.nome },
                { label: "Slug", value: locatario.slug },
                {
                  label: "ID técnico",
                  value: locatario.locatarioId,
                  technical: true,
                },
                { label: "Status", value: statusText(locatario.status) },
              ]}
            />
          </SuperAdminCard>

          <SuperAdminCard
            title="Contato e operação"
            description="Dados administrativos usados pela plataforma."
          >
            <SuperAdminDetailList
              items={[
                { label: "E-mail", value: locatario.email },
                { label: "Documento", value: locatario.documento },
                { label: "Fuso horário", value: locatario.fusoHorario },
              ]}
            />
          </SuperAdminCard>
        </section>

        <section className="ck-superadmin-detail-grid">
          <SuperAdminCard title="Datas">
            <SuperAdminDetailList
              items={[
                { label: "Criado em", value: formatDateTime(locatario.criadoEm) },
                {
                  label: "Atualizado em",
                  value: formatDateTime(locatario.atualizadoEm),
                },
                {
                  label: "Excluído em",
                  value: formatDateTime(locatario.excluidoEm),
                },
              ]}
            />
          </SuperAdminCard>

          <SuperAdminCard title="Motivos administrativos">
            <SuperAdminDetailList
              items={[
                {
                  label: "Motivo da suspensão",
                  value: locatario.motivoSuspensao,
                },
                {
                  label: "Motivo do encerramento",
                  value: locatario.motivoExclusao,
                },
              ]}
            />
          </SuperAdminCard>
        </section>

        <SuperAdminCard
          title="Ações administrativas"
          description="Use com cautela. Estas ações alteram o ciclo de vida do locatário."
        >
          {erroAcao ? <p>{erroAcao}</p> : null}

          <div className="ck-superadmin-page__actions">
            <SuperAdminButton
              type="button"
              disabled={Boolean(isRunning)}
              onClick={() => executarAcao("suspender")}
            >
              {isRunning === "suspender" ? "Suspendendo..." : "Suspender"}
            </SuperAdminButton>

            <SuperAdminButton
              type="button"
              disabled={Boolean(isRunning)}
              onClick={() => executarAcao("reativar")}
            >
              {isRunning === "reativar" ? "Reativando..." : "Reativar"}
            </SuperAdminButton>

            <SuperAdminButton
              type="button"
              disabled={Boolean(isRunning)}
              onClick={() => executarAcao("encerrar")}
            >
              {isRunning === "encerrar" ? "Encerrando..." : "Encerrar"}
            </SuperAdminButton>
          </div>
        </SuperAdminCard>
      </>
    );
  }

  return (
    <SuperAdminPage
      kicker="Plataforma"
      title={item?.nome ?? "Locatário"}
      description={
        item
          ? `${item.slug} — ${statusText(item.status)}`
          : "Detalhes administrativos do locatário."
      }
      actions={
        item ? (
          <>
            <SuperAdminButton
              to={`/plataforma/locatarios/${item.locatarioId}/editar`}
              variant="primary"
            >
              Editar
            </SuperAdminButton>

            <SuperAdminButton
              to={`/plataforma/locatarios/${item.locatarioId}/usuarios`}
            >
              Usuários
            </SuperAdminButton>

            <SuperAdminButton to="/plataforma/locatarios">
              Lista de locatários
            </SuperAdminButton>
          </>
        ) : (
          <SuperAdminButton to="/plataforma/locatarios">
            Lista de locatários
          </SuperAdminButton>
        )
      }
    >
      {isLoading ? <SuperAdminCard>Carregando locatário...</SuperAdminCard> : null}

      {erro ? <SuperAdminCard title="Erro">{erro}</SuperAdminCard> : null}

      {!isLoading && !erro && item ? renderDetails(item) : null}

      {!isLoading && !erro && !item ? (
        <SuperAdminCard>Locatário não encontrado.</SuperAdminCard>
      ) : null}
    </SuperAdminPage>
  );
}


===== apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/pages/LocatariosListPage.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/pages/LocatariosListPage.tsx

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { mapError } from "../../../../core/errors/mapError";
import { SuperAdminButton, SuperAdminCard, SuperAdminPage } from "../../shared";
import { locatariosApi } from "../api/locatariosApi";
import { LocatariosTable } from "../components/LocatariosTable";
import type { Locatario } from "../tipos";

export default function LocatariosListPage() {
  const [itens, setItens] = useState<Locatario[]>([]);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [erro, setErro] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    let ativo = true;

    async function carregar() {
      setIsLoading(true);
      setErro(null);

      try {
        const response = await locatariosApi.listar();

        if (ativo) {
          setItens(response.itens);
        }
      } catch (error) {
        if (ativo) {
          setErro(mapError(error).message);
        }
      } finally {
        if (ativo) {
          setIsLoading(false);
        }
      }
    }

    void carregar();

    return () => {
      ativo = false;
    };
  }, []);

  return (
    <SuperAdminPage
      kicker="Plataforma"
      title="Locatários"
      description="Gerencie empresas, tenants, usuários vinculados e configurações globais."
      actions={
        <SuperAdminButton to="/plataforma/locatarios/novo" variant="primary">
          Novo locatário
        </SuperAdminButton>
      }
    >
      {erro ? (
        <SuperAdminCard title="Erro ao carregar locatários">
          {erro}
        </SuperAdminCard>
      ) : null}

      {isLoading ? (
        <SuperAdminCard>
          Carregando locatários...
        </SuperAdminCard>
      ) : (
        <LocatariosTable itens={itens} />
      )}
    </SuperAdminPage>
  );
}

===== apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/pages/NovoUsuarioLocatarioPage.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/pages/NovoUsuarioLocatarioPage.tsx

import { useState } from "react";
import { Link, useNavigate, useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { mapError } from "../../../../core/errors/mapError";
import { UsuarioForm } from "../../../tenant-admin/usuarios";
import type { AtualizarUsuarioInput, CriarUsuarioInput } from "../../../tenant-admin/usuarios";
import { usuariosLocatarioApi } from "../api/usuariosLocatarioApi";

export default function NovoUsuarioLocatarioPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const params = useParams<{ locatarioId: string }>();
  const locatarioId = params.locatarioId?.trim();
  const [isSaving, setIsSaving] = useState(false);
  const [erro, setErro] = useState<string | null>(null);
  async function handleSubmit(input: CriarUsuarioInput | AtualizarUsuarioInput) {
    if (!locatarioId) { setErro("ID do locatário inválido."); return; }
    setIsSaving(true); setErro(null);
    try { await usuariosLocatarioApi.criar(locatarioId, input as CriarUsuarioInput); navigate(`/plataforma/locatarios/${locatarioId}`, { replace: true }); }
    catch (error) { setErro(mapError(error).message); }
    finally { setIsSaving(false); }
  }
  if (!locatarioId) return <div className="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700">ID do locatário inválido.</div>;
  return <div className="grid gap-6"><div><h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold">Novo usuário do locatário</h1><p className="text-sm text-slate-600">Cadastre um usuário diretamente pela área da plataforma.</p></div><div><Link className="text-blue-600 underline" to={`/plataforma/locatarios/${locatarioId}`}>Voltar ao locatário</Link></div><UsuarioForm mode="create" isSaving={isSaving} erro={erro} onSubmit={handleSubmit} /></div>;
}

// apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/pages/UsuariosLocatarioListPage.tsx

import { Link, useParams } from "react-router-dom";

export default function UsuariosLocatarioListPage() {

        ID do locatário inválido.
      </div>

  return (

          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
          <button
            className="rounded-xl border px-4 py-2"
            type="button"
            onClick={() => void recarregar()}

          </button>

          <Link
            className="rounded-xl border px-4 py-2"
            to={`/plataforma/locatarios/${locatarioId}`}
          >
            Voltar ao locatário

            className="rounded-xl border px-4 py-2"
            to={`/plataforma/locatarios/${locatarioId}/usuarios/novo`}
          >
            Novo usuário
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>

      {erro ? (
        <div className="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700">
          {erro}
        </div>
      ) : null}

      {isLoading ? (
        <div className="rounded-2xl border bg-white p-6">
          Carregando usuários...
        </div>
      ) : (
        <UsuariosLocatarioTable itens={itens} />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


===== apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/tipos.ts =====

// apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/tipos.ts

export type StatusLocatario =
  | "ATIVO"
  | "SUSPENSO"
  | "ENCERRADO"
  | "INATIVO";

export type ModoTema = "SISTEMA" | "CLARO" | "ESCURO";

export type Locatario = {
  locatarioId: string;
  nome: string;
  slug: string;
  email: string;
  documento?: string | null;
  fusoHorario?: string | null;
  status: StatusLocatario;
  motivoSuspensao?: string | null;
  motivoExclusao?: string | null;
  criadoEm?: string;
  atualizadoEm?: string;
  excluidoEm?: string | null;
};

export type ConfiguracaoLocatario = {
  configuracaoId: string;
  locatarioId: string;
  modoTema?: ModoTema;
  identidadeVisual: string;
  corPrimaria?: string | null;
  corSecundaria?: string | null;
  corTerciaria?: string | null;
  urlLogo?: string | null;
  ativa: boolean;
  criadoEm?: string;
  atualizadoEm?: string;
};

export type CriarLocatarioInput = {
  nome: string;
  slug: string;
  email: string;
  documento?: string | null;
  fusoHorario?: string;
  configuracaoInicial: {
    modoTema?: ModoTema;
    identidadeVisual: string;
    corPrimaria?: string | null;
    corSecundaria?: string | null;
    corTerciaria?: string | null;
    urlLogo?: string | null;
    ativa: boolean;
  };
};

export type AtualizarLocatarioInput = {
  nome?: string;
  email?: string;
  documento?: string | null;
  fusoHorario?: string;
};

export type SuspenderLocatarioInput = {
  motivoSuspensao?: string | null;
};

export type ReativarLocatarioInput = {
  motivoReativacao?: string | null;
};

export type EncerrarLocatarioInput = {
  motivoExclusao?: string | null;
};

export type ListarLocatariosResponse = {
  itens: Locatario[];
};

export type ObterLocatarioResponse = {
  item: Locatario;
};

export type CriarLocatarioResponse = {
  item: {
    locatarioId: string;
  };
};

export type AtualizarLocatarioResponse = {
  item: Locatario;
};

export type AcaoLocatarioResponse = {
  item: Locatario;
};

===== apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/usuarios/components/UsuariosLocatarioTable.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/usuarios/components/UsuariosLocatarioTable.tsx

import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import "../../../shared/superAdminPages.css";

export type UsuarioLocatarioResumo = {
  usuarioId: string;
  nome?: string | null;
  email?: string | null;
  ativo?: boolean | null;
};

type Props = {
  itens: UsuarioLocatarioResumo[];
  locatarioId: string;
};

function statusLabel(ativo?: boolean | null): string {
  return ativo === false ? "Inativo" : "Ativo";
}

export function UsuariosLocatarioTable({
  itens,
  locatarioId,
}: Props) {
  if (itens.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="ck-superadmin-card">
        Nenhum usuário encontrado para este locatário.
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="ck-superadmin-table-panel">
      <table className="ck-superadmin-table">
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>Usuário</th>
            <th>E-mail</th>
            <th>Status</th>
            <th>ID técnico</th>
            <th>Ações</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>

        <tbody>
          {itens.map((usuario) => (
            <tr key={usuario.usuarioId}>
              <td>{usuario.nome ?? "Sem nome"}</td>
              <td>{usuario.email ?? "—"}</td>
              <td>{statusLabel(usuario.ativo)}</td>
              <td className="ck-superadmin-technical">
                {usuario.usuarioId}
              </td>
              <td>
                <div className="ck-superadmin-row-actions">
                  <Link
                    className="ck-superadmin-button"
                    to={`/plataforma/locatarios/${locatarioId}/usuarios/${usuario.usuarioId}`}
                  >
                    Ver
                  </Link>

                  <Link
                    className="ck-superadmin-button"
                    to={`/plataforma/locatarios/${locatarioId}/usuarios/${usuario.usuarioId}/editar`}
                  >
                    Editar
                  </Link>
                </div>
              </td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
}

                placeholder="Senha temporária"
              />
            </label>
          </div>

        <div className="ck-superadmin-page__actions">
          <SuperAdminButton type="submit" variant="primary" disabled>
            Salvar indisponível nesta etapa
          </SuperAdminButton>
        </div>
      </form>
    </SuperAdminPage>

}

===== apps/web/src/features/superadmin/shared/SuperAdminButton.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/features/superadmin/shared/superAdmin-ui/SuperAdminButton.tsx

import type { ButtonHTMLAttributes, JSX, ReactNode } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";

type CommonProps = {
  children: ReactNode;
  variant?: "default" | "primary";
  className?: string;
};

type LinkButtonProps = CommonProps & {
  to: string;
};

type NativeButtonProps = CommonProps &
  ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> & {
    to?: undefined;
  };

function buttonClassName(variant: CommonProps["variant"], extra = "") {
  return `ck-superadmin-button ${
    variant === "primary" ? "ck-superadmin-button--primary" : ""
  } ${extra}`.trim();
}

export function SuperAdminButton(
  props: LinkButtonProps | NativeButtonProps,
): JSX.Element {
  const { children, variant = "default", className = "" } = props;

  if ("to" in props && props.to) {
    return (
      <Link className={buttonClassName(variant, className)} to={props.to}>
        {children}
      </Link>
    );
  }

  const { to: _to, ...buttonProps } = props;

  return (
    <button
      {...buttonProps}
      className={buttonClassName(variant, className)}
    >
      {children}
    </button>
  );
}


===== apps/web/src/features/superadmin/shared/SuperAdminCard.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/features/superadmin/shared/superAdmin-ui/SuperAdminCard.tsx

import type { JSX, ReactNode } from "react";

type Props = {
  children: ReactNode;
  title?: ReactNode;
  description?: ReactNode;
};

export function SuperAdminCard({
  children,
  title,
  description,
}: Props): JSX.Element {
  return (
    <section className="ck-superadmin-card">
      {title || description ? (
        <div className="ck-superadmin-card__title">
          {title ? <h2>{title}</h2> : null}
          {description ? <p>{description}</p> : null}
        </div>
      ) : null}

      {children}
    </section>
  );
}

===== apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/assinatura/api/assinaturaModulosApi.ts =====

// apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/assinatura/api/assinaturaModulosApi.ts

import { httpClient } from "../../../../core/api/httpClient";
import type { DesvincularModuloAssinaturaResponse, ListarMeusModulosAssinaturaResponse, VincularModuloAssinaturaInput, VincularModuloAssinaturaResponse } from "../tipos";

const BASE = "/me/assinatura/modulos";

export const assinaturaModulosApi = {
  listar(): Promise<ListarMeusModulosAssinaturaResponse> {
    return httpClient.get<ListarMeusModulosAssinaturaResponse>(BASE);
  },

  vincular(
    input: VincularModuloAssinaturaInput,
  ): Promise<VincularModuloAssinaturaResponse> {
    return httpClient.post<
      VincularModuloAssinaturaResponse,
      VincularModuloAssinaturaInput
    >(BASE, input);
  },

  desvincular(moduloId: string): Promise<DesvincularModuloAssinaturaResponse> {
    return httpClient.delete<DesvincularModuloAssinaturaResponse>(
      `${BASE}/${moduloId}`,
    );
  },
};

===== apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/assinatura/components/AssinaturaModulosTable.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/assinatura/components/AssinaturaModulosTable.tsx

import type { ModuloAssinatura } from "../tipos";

type Props = {
  modulos: ModuloAssinatura[];
  podeAtualizar?: boolean;
  isRunning?: boolean;
  onDesvincular?: (moduloCodigo: string) => void;
};

export function AssinaturaModulosTable({ modulos, podeAtualizar, isRunning, onDesvincular }: Props) {
  if (modulos.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-xl border bg-white p-4 text-sm text-slate-600">
        Nenhum módulo habilitado nesta assinatura.
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-2xl border bg-white">
      <table className="min-w-full border-collapse text-sm">
        <thead>
          <tr className="border-b bg-slate-50 text-left">
            <th className="px-4 py-3">Código</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-3">Origem</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-3">Ações</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>

        <tbody>
          {modulos.map((modulo) => (
            <tr key={modulo.codigo} className="border-b last:border-b-0">
              <td className="px-4 py-3">{modulo.codigo}</td>
              <td className="px-4 py-3">{modulo.origem}</td>
              <td className="px-4 py-3">
                {podeAtualizar ? (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className="text-red-700 underline"
                    disabled={Boolean(isRunning)}
                    onClick={() => onDesvincular?.(modulo.codigo)}
                  >
                    Desvincular
                  </button>
                ) : (
                  <span className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                    Sem permissão de alteração
                  </span>
                )}
              </td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
}

===== apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/assinatura/components/VincularModuloForm.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/assinatura/components/VincularModuloForm.tsx

import { useState } from "react";

type Props = {
  isSaving?: boolean;
  onSubmit: (moduloId: string) => void | Promise<void>;
};

export function VincularModuloForm({ isSaving, onSubmit }: Props) {
  const [moduloId, setModuloId] = useState("");

  async function handleSubmit(event: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();

    await onSubmit(moduloId);
    setModuloId("");
  }

  return (
    <form
      className="grid gap-4 rounded-2xl border bg-white p-6"
      onSubmit={handleSubmit}
    >
      <div>
        <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">Vincular módulo</h2>
        <p className="text-sm text-slate-600">
          Informe o ID do módulo a ser habilitado na assinatura.
        </p>
      </div>

      <label className="grid gap-1">
        <span className="text-sm font-medium">ID do módulo</span>
        <input
          className="rounded-xl border px-3 py-2"
          value={moduloId}
          onChange={(event) => setModuloId(event.target.value)}
          required
        />
      </label>

      <div className="flex justify-end">
        <button
          className="rounded-xl border px-4 py-2 font-medium"
          type="submit"
          disabled={Boolean(isSaving)}
        >
          {isSaving ? "Vinculando..." : "Vincular"}
        </button>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}

===== apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/assinatura/hooks/useAssinaturaModuloActions.ts =====

// apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/assinatura/hooks/useAssinaturaModuloActions.ts

import { useState } from "react";
import { mapError } from "../../../../core/errors/mapError";
import { assinaturaModulosApi } from "../api/assinaturaModulosApi";

type ActionName = "vincular" | "desvincular";

type Params = {
  onSuccess?: () => void | Promise<void>;
};

export function useAssinaturaModuloActions({ onSuccess }: Params = {}) {
  const [isRunning, setIsRunning] = useState<ActionName | null>(null);
  const [erro, setErro] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [sucesso, setSucesso] = useState<string | null>(null);

  async function vincular(moduloId: string) {
    const id = moduloId.trim();

    if (!id) {
      setErro("Informe o ID do módulo.");
      return false;
    }

    setIsRunning("vincular");
    setErro(null);
    setSucesso(null);

    try {
      await assinaturaModulosApi.vincular({ moduloId: id });
      setSucesso("Módulo vinculado com sucesso.");
      await onSuccess?.();
      return true;
    } catch (error) {
      setErro(mapError(error).message);
      return false;
    } finally {
      setIsRunning(null);
    }
  }

  async function desvincular(moduloId: string) {
    const id = moduloId.trim();

    if (!id) {
      setErro("Módulo inválido.");
      return false;
    }

    setIsRunning("desvincular");
    setErro(null);
    setSucesso(null);

    try {
      await assinaturaModulosApi.desvincular(id);
      setSucesso("Módulo desvinculado com sucesso.");
      await onSuccess?.();
      return true;
    } catch (error) {
      setErro(mapError(error).message);
      return false;
    } finally {
      setIsRunning(null);
    }
  }

  return {
    vincular,
    desvincular,
    isRunning,
    erro,
    sucesso,
  };
}

===== apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/assinatura/hooks/useAssinaturaModulos.ts =====

// apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/assinatura/hooks/useAssinaturaModulos.ts

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { mapError } from "../../../../core/errors/mapError";
import { assinaturaModulosApi } from "../api/assinaturaModulosApi";
import type { ModuloAssinatura } from "../tipos";

export function useAssinaturaModulos() {
  const [locatarioId, setLocatarioId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [modulos, setModulos] = useState<ModuloAssinatura[]>([]);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [erro, setErro] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const carregar = useCallback(async () => {
    setIsLoading(true);
    setErro(null);

    try {
      const response = await assinaturaModulosApi.listar();
      setLocatarioId(response.locatarioId);
      setModulos(response.modulos);
    } catch (error) {
      setModulos([]);
      setErro(mapError(error).message);
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    void carregar();
  }, [carregar]);

  return {
    locatarioId,
    modulos,
    isLoading,
    erro,
    recarregar: carregar,
  };
}

===== apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/assinatura/pages/AssinaturaModulosPage.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/features/tenant-admin/assinatura/pages/AssinaturaModulosPage.tsx

import { useAuthorization } from "../../../../core/auth/useAuthorization";
import { VincularModuloForm } from "../components/VincularModuloForm";
import { useAssinaturaModuloActions } from "../hooks/useAssinaturaModuloActions";
import { useAssinaturaModulos } from "../hooks/useAssinaturaModulos";

const ASSINATURAS_MODULE = "ASSINATURAS";

const ASSINATURAS_PERMISSIONS = {
  ATUALIZAR: "tenant.assinatura.atualizar",
} as const;

type ModuloAssinatura = {
  codigo: string;
  origem?: string | null;
};

type AssinaturaModulosTableProps = {
  modulos: ModuloAssinatura[];
  podeAtualizar: boolean;
  isRunning: boolean;
  onDesvincular: (moduloId: string) => void;
};

function AssinaturaModulosTableLocal({
  modulos,
  podeAtualizar,
  isRunning,
  onDesvincular,
}: AssinaturaModulosTableProps) {
  if (modulos.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-xl border bg-white p-4 text-sm text-slate-600">
        Nenhum módulo habilitado para este locatário.
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-2xl border bg-white">
      <table className="min-w-full border-collapse text-sm">
        <thead>
          <tr className="border-b bg-slate-50 text-left">
            <th className="px-4 py-3">Código</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-3">Origem</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-3">Ações</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>

        <tbody>
          {modulos.map((modulo) => (
            <tr key={modulo.codigo} className="border-b last:border-b-0">
              <td className="px-4 py-3 font-medium">{modulo.codigo}</td>
              <td className="px-4 py-3">{modulo.origem ?? "—"}</td>
              <td className="px-4 py-3">
                {podeAtualizar ? (
                  <button
                    className="text-red-600 underline disabled:opacity-50"
                    type="button"
                    disabled={isRunning}
                    onClick={() => onDesvincular(modulo.codigo)}
                  >
                    Desvincular
                  </button>
                ) : (
                  <span className="text-slate-500">Sem permissão</span>
                )}
              </td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function AssinaturaModulosPage() {
  const { hasPermission, hasModule } = useAuthorization();

  const {
    locatarioId,
    modulos,
    isLoading,
    erro,
    recarregar,
  } = useAssinaturaModulos();

  const {
    vincular,
    desvincular,
    isRunning,
    erro: erroAcao,
    sucesso,
  } = useAssinaturaModuloActions({
    onSuccess: recarregar,
  });

  const podeAtualizar = hasModule() && hasPermission(ASSINATURAS_PERMISSIONS.ATUALIZAR);

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="rounded-2xl border bg-white p-6">
        Carregando módulos da assinatura...
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="grid gap-6">
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold">Módulos da assinatura</h1>
        <p className="text-sm text-slate-600">
          Consulte os módulos habilitados para o locatário.
        </p>
      </div>

      {locatarioId ? (
        <div className="rounded-2xl border bg-white p-4 text-sm">
          <strong>Locatário:</strong> {locatarioId}
        </div>
      ) : null}

      {erro ? (
        <div className="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700">
          {erro}
        </div>
      ) : null}

      {erroAcao ? (
        <div className="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700">
          {erroAcao}
        </div>
      ) : null}

      {sucesso ? (
        <div className="rounded-xl border border-green-200 bg-green-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-green-700">
          {sucesso}
        </div>
      ) : null}

      <div className="flex gap-3">
        <button
          className="rounded-xl border px-4 py-2"
          type="button"
          onClick={() => void recarregar()}
        >
          Recarregar
        </button>
      </div>

      <AssinaturaModulosTableLocal
        modulos={modulos}
        podeAtualizar={podeAtualizar}
        isRunning={isRunning !== null}
        onDesvincular={(moduloId) => void desvincular(moduloId)}
      />

      {podeAtualizar ? (
        <VincularModuloForm
          isSaving={isRunning === "vincular"}
          onSubmit={(moduloId) => void vincular(moduloId)}
        />
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
