===== apps/web/src/shared/domain-selects/ClienteSelect.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/shared/domain-selects/ClienteSelect.tsx

import type { JSX } from "react";
import type { Cliente } from "../../features/tenant-admin/clientes/tipos";
import { formatClienteOptionLabel } from "../formatters";

type ClienteSelectProps = {
  value: string;
  clientes: Cliente[];
  disabled?: boolean;
  allowEmpty?: boolean;
  emptyLabel?: string;
  className?: string;
  onChange: (clienteId: string) => void;
};

export function ClienteSelect({
  value,
  clientes,
  disabled = false,
  allowEmpty = true,
  emptyLabel = "Selecione um cliente",
  className = "ck-register-select",
  onChange,
}: ClienteSelectProps): JSX.Element {
  return (
    <select
      className={className}
      value={value}
      disabled={disabled}
      onChange={(event) => onChange(event.target.value)}
    >
      {allowEmpty ? <option value="">{emptyLabel}</option> : null}

      {clientes.map((cliente) => (
        <option key={cliente.clienteId} value={cliente.clienteId}>
          {formatClienteOptionLabel(cliente)}
        </option>
      ))}
    </select>
  );
}


===== apps/web/src/shared/domain-selects/FornecedorSelect.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/shared/domain-selects/FornecedorSelect.tsx

import type { JSX } from "react";
import type { Fornecedor } from "../../features/tenant-admin/fornecedores/tipos";
import { formatFornecedorOptionLabel } from "../formatters";

type FornecedorSelectProps = {
  value: string;
  fornecedores: Fornecedor[];
  disabled?: boolean;
  allowEmpty?: boolean;
  emptyLabel?: string;
  className?: string;
  onChange: (fornecedorId: string) => void;
};

export function FornecedorSelect({
  value,
  fornecedores,
  disabled = false,
  allowEmpty = true,
  emptyLabel = "Selecione um fornecedor",
  className = "ck-register-select",
  onChange,
}: FornecedorSelectProps): JSX.Element {
  return (
    <select
      className={className}
      value={value}
      disabled={disabled}
      onChange={(event) => onChange(event.target.value)}
    >
      {allowEmpty ? <option value="">{emptyLabel}</option> : null}

      {fornecedores.map((fornecedor) => (
        <option key={fornecedor.fornecedorId} value={fornecedor.fornecedorId}>
          {formatFornecedorOptionLabel(fornecedor)}
        </option>
      ))}
    </select>
  );
}

import type { JSX } from "react";

type TransacaoOption = {
  transacaoId: string;
  tipo?: string | null;
  status?: string | null;
  criadoEm?: string | null;
  data?: string | null;
  clienteNome?: string | null;
  fornecedorNome?: string | null;
  pessoaNome?: string | null;
  valorTotal?: number | string | null;
};

type TransacaoSelectProps<TTransacao extends TransacaoOption = TransacaoOption> = {
  value: string;
  transacoes: TTransacao[];
  disabled?: boolean;
  allowEmpty?: boolean;
  emptyLabel?: string;
  className?: string;
  onChange: (transacaoId: string) => void;
};

export function TransacaoSelect<TTransacao extends TransacaoOption = TransacaoOption>({
  value,
  transacoes,
  disabled = false,
  allowEmpty = true,
  emptyLabel = "Selecione uma transação",
  className = "ck-register-select",
  onChange,
}: TransacaoSelectProps<TTransacao>): JSX.Element {
  return (
    <select
      className={className}
      value={value}
      disabled={disabled}
      onChange={(event) => onChange(event.target.value)}

      {allowEmpty ? <option value="">{emptyLabel}</option> : null}

        <option key={transacao.transacaoId} value={transacao.transacaoId}>

import { cleanText } from "./commonLabel";

export function formatClienteContato(cliente: any): string {
  return cliente.documento ?? cliente.telefone ?? cliente.email ?? "sem contato";
}

export function formatClienteLabel(cliente: any): string {
  return `${cleanText(cliente.nome) ?? "Cliente sem nome"} — ${formatClienteContato(cliente)}`;
}

export const formatClienteOptionLabel = formatClienteLabel;


===== apps/web/src/shared/formatters/commonLabel.ts =====


export function cleanText(value?: string | null): string | null {
  const text = value?.trim();
  return text ? text : null;
}

export function joinLabelParts(parts: Array<string | null | undefined>, separator = " — "): string {
  return parts.filter(Boolean).join(separator);
}

export function shortCode(value?: string | null, length = 8): string {
  if (!value) {
    return "-";
  }

  return value.slice(0, length).toUpperCase();
}


===== apps/web/src/shared/formatters/dateTime.ts =====

// apps/web/src/shared/formatters/dateTime.ts

export function formatDateTime(value?: string | null): string {
  if (!value) return "—";

  const date = new Date(value);
  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return value;

  return date.toLocaleString("pt-BR", {
    day: "2-digit",
    month: "2-digit",
    year: "numeric",
    hour: "2-digit",
    minute: "2-digit",
  });
}

export function formatDate(value?: string | null): string {
  if (!value) return "—";

  const date = new Date(value);
  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return value;

  return date.toLocaleDateString("pt-BR");
}


===== apps/web/src/shared/formatters/domainLabels.ts =====

// apps/web/src/shared/formatters/domainLabels.ts

type ClienteLike = {
  nome?: string | null;
  documento?: string | null;
  cpf?: string | null;
  cnpj?: string | null;
  telefone?: string | null;
  email?: string | null;
};

type FornecedorLike = {
  nome?: string | null;
  documento?: string | null;
  cpf?: string | null;
  cnpj?: string | null;
  telefone?: string | null;
  email?: string | null;
};

type ProdutoLike = {
  nome?: string | null;
  codigo?: string | null;
  sku?: string | null;
  unidade?: string | null;
  tipo?: string | null;
};

type UsuarioLike = {
  nome?: string | null;
  email?: string | null;
};

type LocatarioLike = {
  nome?: string | null;
  nomeFantasia?: string | null;
  slug?: string | null;
};

type TransacaoLike = {
  tipo?: string | null;
  status?: string | null;
  criadoEm?: string | null;
  data?: string | null;
  clienteNome?: string | null;
  fornecedorNome?: string | null;
  pessoaNome?: string | null;
  valorTotal?: number | string | null;
};

function clean(value?: string | null): string | null {
  const trimmed = value?.trim();
  return trimmed ? trimmed : null;
}

/**
 * Mantido para áreas técnicas, especialmente superadmin, auditoria e suporte.
 * Evite usar este helper como label visual principal em telas tenant.
 */
export function shortCode(id?: string | null, size = 8): string {
  const value = clean(id);
  return value ? value.slice(0, size).toUpperCase() : "—";
}

function formatDateTimeLabel(value?: string | null): string | null {
  const raw = clean(value);
  if (!raw) return null;

  const date = new Date(raw);
  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return raw;

  return date.toLocaleString("pt-BR", {
    day: "2-digit",
    month: "2-digit",
    year: "numeric",
    hour: "2-digit",
    minute: "2-digit",
  });
}

function formatMoneyLabel(value?: number | string | null): string | null {
  if (value === null || value === undefined || value === "") return null;

  const numberValue = typeof value === "number" ? value : Number(value);

  if (Number.isNaN(numberValue)) {
    return String(value);
  }

  return numberValue.toLocaleString("pt-BR", {
    style: "currency",
    currency: "BRL",
  });
}

function joinLabelParts(parts: Array<string | null | undefined>, separator = " — "): string {
  return parts.filter((part): part is string => Boolean(clean(part))).join(separator);
}

function documentoPessoa(pessoa?: ClienteLike | FornecedorLike | null): string | null {
  return clean(pessoa?.documento) ?? clean(pessoa?.cpf) ?? clean(pessoa?.cnpj);
}

function contatoPessoa(pessoa?: ClienteLike | FornecedorLike | null): string | null {
  return documentoPessoa(pessoa) ?? clean(pessoa?.telefone) ?? clean(pessoa?.email);
}

export function formatClienteLabel(cliente?: ClienteLike | null): string {
  if (!cliente) return "Cliente não informado";

  const nome = clean(cliente.nome) ?? "Cliente sem nome";
  const contato = contatoPessoa(cliente);

  return contato ? `${nome} — ${contato}` : `${nome} — sem contato`;
}

export function formatClienteOptionLabel(cliente?: ClienteLike | null): string {
  return formatClienteLabel(cliente);
}

export function formatFornecedorLabel(fornecedor?: FornecedorLike | null): string {
  if (!fornecedor) return "Fornecedor não informado";

  const nome = clean(fornecedor.nome) ?? "Fornecedor sem nome";
  const contato = contatoPessoa(fornecedor);

  return contato ? `${nome} — ${contato}` : `${nome} — sem contato`;
}

export function formatFornecedorOptionLabel(fornecedor?: FornecedorLike | null): string {
  return formatFornecedorLabel(fornecedor);
}

export function formatProdutoLabel(produto?: ProdutoLike | null): string {
  if (!produto) return "Produto não informado";

  const nome = clean(produto.nome) ?? "Produto sem nome";
  const codigo = clean(produto.codigo) ?? clean(produto.sku);
  const unidade = clean(produto.unidade);

  if (codigo && unidade) return `${nome} — Código ${codigo} • ${unidade}`;
  if (codigo) return `${nome} — Código ${codigo}`;
  if (unidade) return `${nome} — ${unidade}`;

  return `${nome} — sem código`;
}

export function formatProdutoOptionLabel(produto?: ProdutoLike | null): string {
  return formatProdutoLabel(produto);
}

export function formatUsuarioLabel(usuario?: UsuarioLike | null): string {
  if (!usuario) return "Usuário não informado";

  const nome = clean(usuario.nome) ?? "Usuário sem nome";
  const email = clean(usuario.email);

  return email ? `${nome} — ${email}` : nome;
}

export function formatLocatarioLabel(locatario?: LocatarioLike | null): string {
  if (!locatario) return "Locatário não informado";

  const nome =
    clean(locatario.nomeFantasia) ??
    clean(locatario.nome) ??
    "Locatário sem nome";
  const slug = clean(locatario.slug);

  return slug ? `${nome} — ${slug}` : nome;
}

export function formatTransacaoTitulo(params: {
  tipo?: string | null;
  criadoEm?: string | null;
  fallbackId?: string | null;
}): string {
  const tipo = clean(params.tipo) ?? "Transação";
  const dataHora = formatDateTimeLabel(params.criadoEm);

  if (dataHora) {
    return `${tipo} • ${dataHora}`;
  }

  // Em tenant, preferimos não exibir fallback com ID.
  return tipo;
}

export function formatTransacaoLabel(transacao?: TransacaoLike | null): string {
  if (!transacao) return "Transação não informada";

  const tipo = clean(transacao.tipo) ?? "Transação";
  const data = formatDateTimeLabel(transacao.criadoEm ?? transacao.data);
  const pessoa =
    clean(transacao.pessoaNome) ??
    clean(transacao.clienteNome) ??
    clean(transacao.fornecedorNome);
  const valor = formatMoneyLabel(transacao.valorTotal);
  const status = clean(transacao.status);

  const principal = joinLabelParts([tipo, data]);
  const secundario = joinLabelParts([pessoa, valor, status], " • ");

  return secundario ? `${principal} — ${secundario}` : principal;
}

export function formatTransacaoOptionLabel(transacao?: TransacaoLike | null): string {
  return formatTransacaoLabel(transacao);
}

export function formatOrdemServicoTitulo(params: {
  numero?: string | number | null;
  titulo?: string | null;
  criadoEm?: string | null;
  fallbackId?: string | null;
}): string {
  if (params.numero !== null && params.numero !== undefined && String(params.numero).trim()) {
    return `OS nº ${params.numero}`;
  }

  const titulo = clean(params.titulo);
  if (titulo) return titulo;

  const dataHora = formatDateTimeLabel(params.criadoEm);
  if (dataHora) return `OS • ${dataHora}`;

  // Em tenant, preferimos não exibir fallback com ID.
  return "Ordem de serviço";
}

===== apps/web/src/shared/layout/AppBreadcrumb.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/shared/layout/AppBreadcrumb.tsx

import type { JSX } from "react";
import { Link, useLocation } from "react-router-dom";

type AppBreadcrumbProps = {
  variant: "tenant" | "plataforma";
};

const LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  app: "Início",
  plataforma: "Plataforma",
  usuarios: "Usuários",
  locatario: "Locatário",
  locatarios: "Locatários",
  configuracoes: "Configurações",
  assinatura: "Assinatura",
  modulos: "Módulos",
  clientes: "Clientes",
  fornecedores: "Fornecedores",
  produtos: "Produtos",
  transacoes: "Transações",
  financeiro: "Financeiro",
  vendas: "Vendas",
  compras: "Compras",
  nova: "Nova",
  novo: "Novo",
  editar: "Editar",
  "minha-senha": "Minha senha",
};

function isLikelyId(segment: string): boolean {
  return (
    segment.length >= 16 ||
    /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}/i.test(segment)
  );
}

function formatSegment(segment: string): string {
  const known = LABELS[segment];

  if (known) return known;

  if (isLikelyId(segment)) {
    return `#${segment.slice(0, 8).toUpperCase()}`;
  }

  return segment
    .split("-")
    .filter(Boolean)
    .map((part) => part.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + part.slice(1))
    .join(" ");
}

export function AppBreadcrumb({ variant }: AppBreadcrumbProps): JSX.Element {
  const location = useLocation();

  const segments = location.pathname
    .split("/")
    .map((segment) => segment.trim())
    .filter(Boolean);

  const rootPath = variant === "plataforma" ? "/plataforma" : "/app";

  if (segments.length <= 1) {
    return (
      <nav aria-label="Breadcrumb" className="text-sm text-slate-500">
        <span>{variant === "plataforma" ? "Plataforma" : "Início"}</span>
      </nav>
    );
  }

  return (
    <nav aria-label="Breadcrumb" className="text-sm text-slate-500">
      <ol className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        {segments.map((segment, index) => {
          const path = `/${segments.slice(0, index + 1).join("/")}`;
          const isLast = index === segments.length - 1;
          const label =
            index === 0
              ? variant === "plataforma"
                ? "Plataforma"
                : "Início"
              : formatSegment(segment);

          if (index === 0 && path !== rootPath) {
            return null;
          }

          return (
            <li key={`${path}-${segment}`} className="flex items-center gap-2">
              {index > 0 ? <span className="text-slate-300">/</span> : null}

              {isLast ? (
                <span className="font-medium text-slate-700">{label}</span>
              ) : (
                <Link className="hover:text-slate-900 hover:underline" to={path}>
                  {label}
                </Link>
              )}
            </li>
          );
        })}
      </ol>
    </nav>
  );
}

===== apps/web/src/shared/layout/AppHeader.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/shared/layout/AppHeader.tsx

import type { JSX } from "react";
import { NavLink } from "react-router-dom";
import { useAuth } from "../../core/auth/useAuth";
import { useTenant } from "../../core/tenant/useTenant";

export type AppNavItem = {
  label: string;
  to: string;
  end?: boolean;
  icon?: string;
};

type AppHeaderProps = {
  variant: "tenant" | "plataforma";
  navItems: AppNavItem[];
};

const APP_ENV = import.meta.env.VITE_APP_ENV ?? "Beta";

function userLabel(user: ReturnType<typeof useAuth>["user"]): string {
  return (
    user?.nome?.trim() ||
    user?.email?.trim() ||
    user?.usuarioId?.slice(0, 8) ||
    "Usuário"
  );
}

function userInitial(user: ReturnType<typeof useAuth>["user"]): string {
  const label = userLabel(user).trim();
  return label.charAt(0).toUpperCase() || "U";
}

export function AppHeader({ variant, navItems }: AppHeaderProps): JSX.Element {
  const { user, logout } = useAuth();
  const { branding } = useTenant();

  const isPlataforma = variant === "plataforma";

  const title = isPlataforma
    ? "Core-kit Plataforma"
    : branding?.nomeFantasia?.trim() || "Core-kit";

  const subtitle = isPlataforma
    ? "Painel de administração do SaaS"
    : "Painel operacional do locatário";

  async function handleLogout() {
    await logout();
  }

  return (
    <header className="ck-header">
      <div className="ck-header-inner">
        <div className="ck-brand">
          {variant === "tenant" && branding?.logoUrl ? (
            <img alt={title} className="ck-brand-logo" src={branding.logoUrl} />
          ) : (
            <div className="ck-brand-mark">CK</div>
          )}

          <div>
            <div className="ck-brand-title-row">
              <strong className="ck-brand-title">{title}</strong>
              <span className="ck-beta-badge">{APP_ENV}</span>
            </div>

            <p className="ck-brand-subtitle">{subtitle}</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="ck-header-actions">
          <div className="ck-search-pill">⌕ Busca global em breve</div>

          <div className="ck-user-card">
            <div className="ck-user-avatar">{userInitial(user)}</div>

            <div className="ck-user-meta">
              <div className="ck-user-name">{userLabel(user)}</div>
              <div className="ck-user-role">
                {isPlataforma ? "Plataforma" : "Locatário"}
              </div>
            </div>

            <button
              className="ck-logout-button"
              type="button"
              onClick={() => void handleLogout()}
            >
              Sair
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <nav className="ck-mobile-nav">
        {navItems.map((item) => (
          <NavLink
            key={item.to}
            className="ck-mobile-nav-link"
            end={item.end}
            to={item.to}
          >
            <span>{item.icon ?? "•"}</span>
            <span>{item.label}</span>
          </NavLink>
        ))}
      </nav>
    </header>
  );
}


===== apps/web/src/shared/layout/AppShell.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/shared/layout/AppShell.tsx

import type { JSX, ReactNode } from "react";

type AppShellProps = {
  children: ReactNode;
  sidebar?: ReactNode;
  header?: ReactNode;
  className?: string;
};

/**
 * Shell genérico preservado por compatibilidade.
 *
 * Usado por áreas como superadmin.
 * Não deve conter regras específicas do tenant.
 */
export function AppShell({
  children,
  sidebar,
  header,
  className = "",
}: AppShellProps): JSX.Element {
  return (
    <div className={`ck-app-shell ${className}`.trim()}>
      {sidebar ? (
        <aside className="ck-app-shell__sidebar">
          {sidebar}
        </aside>
      ) : null}

      <div className="ck-app-shell__body">
        {header ? (
          <header className="ck-app-shell__header">
            {header}
          </header>
        ) : null}

        <main className="ck-app-shell__main">
          {children}
        </main>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// apps/web/src/shared/layout/TenantSidebar.tsx

import type { JSX } from "react";
import { NavLink } from "react-router-dom";
import { useTenantMenuCollapsed } from "./useTenantMenuCollapsed";
import {
  getTenantNavigationIcon,
  type TenantNavigationIconKey,
} from "../navigation";

type TenantMenuItem = {
  label: string;
  to: string;
  iconKey: TenantNavigationIconKey;
};

const MENU_ITEMS: TenantMenuItem[] = [
  { label: "Dashboard", to: "/app", iconKey: "dashboard" },
  { label: "Clientes", to: "/app/clientes", iconKey: "clientes" },
  { label: "Fornecedores", to: "/app/fornecedores", iconKey: "fornecedores" },
  { label: "Produtos", to: "/app/produtos", iconKey: "produtos" },
  { label: "Transações", to: "/app/transacoes", iconKey: "transacoes" },
  { label: "Financeiro", to: "/app/financeiro", iconKey: "financeiro" },
  { label: "Ordens de serviço", to: "/app/ordens-servico", iconKey: "ordensServico", },
];

export function TenantSidebar(): JSX.Element {
  const { collapsed, toggleCollapsed } = useTenantMenuCollapsed();

  return (
    <aside
      className={`ck-tenant-sidebar ${
        collapsed ? "ck-tenant-sidebar--collapsed" : ""
      }`}
    >
      <div className="ck-tenant-sidebar__header">
        <strong className="ck-tenant-sidebar__title">Módulos</strong>

        <button
          type="button"
          className="ck-tenant-sidebar__toggle"
          aria-label="Alternar menu lateral"
          title="Alternar menu lateral"
          onClick={toggleCollapsed}
        >
          ☰
        </button>
      </div>

      <nav className="ck-tenant-sidebar__nav" aria-label="Menu tenant">
        {MENU_ITEMS.map((item) => (
          <NavLink
            key={item.to}
            to={item.to}
            end={item.to === "/app"}
            className={({ isActive }) =>
              `ck-tenant-sidebar__link ${isActive ? "is-active" : ""}`
            }
            title={item.label}
          >
            <span className="ck-tenant-sidebar__icon" aria-hidden="true">
              {getTenantNavigationIcon(item.iconKey)}
            </span>

            <span className="ck-tenant-sidebar__label">{item.label}</span>
          </NavLink>
        ))}
      </nav>
    </aside>
  );
}

===== apps/web/src/shared/layout/useTenantMenuCollapsed.ts =====

// apps/web/src/shared/layout/useTenantMenuCollapsed.ts

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

const STORAGE_KEY = "tenant-menu-collapsed";

export function useTenantMenuCollapsed(defaultValue = true) {
  const [collapsed, setCollapsed] = useState(defaultValue);

  useEffect(() => {
    const stored = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);

    if (stored === null) {
      setCollapsed(true);
      return;
    }

    setCollapsed(stored === "true");
  }, []);

  function toggleCollapsed(): void {
    setCollapsed((current) => {
      const next = !current;
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, String(next));
      return next;
    });
  }

  return {
    collapsed,
    setCollapsed,
    toggleCollapsed,
  };
}

===== apps/web/src/shared/navigation/CollapsibleBreadcrumbs.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/shared/navigation/CollapsibleBreadcrumbs.tsx

import { useState, type JSX } from "react";
import {
  DomainBreadcrumbs,
  type DomainBreadcrumbItem,
} from "./DomainBreadcrumbs";

type CollapsibleBreadcrumbsProps = {
  items: DomainBreadcrumbItem[];
  defaultOpen?: boolean;
  label?: string;
};

/**
 * Breadcrumb retrátil.
 *
 * Estado fechado:
 * - mostra botão de 3 traços;
 * - mostra apenas os ícones do caminho;
 * - no hover/focus, revela linha completa.
 *
 * Estado aberto:
 * - mostra o breadcrumb completo.
 */
export function CollapsibleBreadcrumbs({
  items,
  defaultOpen = false,
  label = "Alternar navegação",
}: CollapsibleBreadcrumbsProps): JSX.Element {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(defaultOpen);

  return (
    <section
      className={`ck-collapsible-breadcrumbs ${
        open ? "ck-collapsible-breadcrumbs--open" : "ck-collapsible-breadcrumbs--collapsed"
      }`.trim()}
    >
      <button
        type="button"
        className="ck-collapsible-breadcrumbs__toggle"
        aria-expanded={open}
        aria-label={label}
        title={label}
        onClick={() => setOpen((current) => !current)}
      >
        <span aria-hidden="true" className="ck-hamburger-icon">
          ☰
        </span>
      </button>

      {open ? (
        <div className="ck-collapsible-breadcrumbs__content ck-collapsible-breadcrumbs__content--full">
          <DomainBreadcrumbs items={items} mode="full" />
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="ck-collapsible-breadcrumbs__content ck-collapsible-breadcrumbs__content--collapsed">
          <div className="ck-collapsible-breadcrumbs__icons">
            <DomainBreadcrumbs items={items} mode="icons" />
          </div>

          <div className="ck-collapsible-breadcrumbs__hover-full">
            <DomainBreadcrumbs items={items} mode="full" />
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}


===== apps/web/src/shared/navigation/DomainBreadcrumbs.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/shared/navigation/DomainBreadcrumbs.tsx

import type { JSX } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import {
  TenantNavigationIcon,
  type TenantNavigationIconKey,
} from "./tenantNavigationIcons";

export type DomainBreadcrumbItem = {
  label: string;
  to?: string;
  iconKey?: TenantNavigationIconKey;
};

type DomainBreadcrumbsProps = {
  items: DomainBreadcrumbItem[];
  ariaLabel?: string;
  mode?: "full" | "icons";
};

function BreadcrumbContent({
  item,
  mode,
}: {
  item: DomainBreadcrumbItem;
  mode: "full" | "icons";
}): JSX.Element | string {
  if (mode === "icons") {
    return <TenantNavigationIcon iconKey={item.iconKey} label={item.label} />;
  }

  return item.label;
}

export function DomainBreadcrumbs({
  items,
  ariaLabel = "Navegação estrutural",
  mode = "full",
}: DomainBreadcrumbsProps): JSX.Element {
  return (
    <nav aria-label={ariaLabel} className={`ck-breadcrumbs ck-breadcrumbs--${mode}`}>
      <ol>
        {items.map((item, index) => {
          const isLast = index === items.length - 1;
          const content = <BreadcrumbContent item={item} mode={mode} />;

          return (
            <li key={`${item.label}-${index}`}>
              {item.to && !isLast ? (
                <Link to={item.to} title={item.label} aria-label={item.label}>
                  {content}
                </Link>
              ) : (
                <span aria-current={isLast ? "page" : undefined} title={item.label}>
                  {content}
                </span>
              )}
            </li>
          );
        })}
      </ol>
    </nav>
  );
}

// apps/web/src/shared/navigation/DomainPageHeader.tsx

import type { JSX, ReactNode } from "react";

// apps/web/src/shared/navigation/tenantNavigationIcons.tsx

import type { JSX } from "react";

export type TenantNavigationIconKey =
  | "inicio"
  | "dashboard"
  | "clientes"
  | "fornecedores"
  | "produtos"
  | "transacoes"
  | "vendas"
  | "compras"
  | "financeiro"
  | "ordensServico"
  | "usuarios"
  | "locatario"
  | "assinatura"
  | "detalhes"
  | "editar"
  | "novo";

export const TENANT_NAVIGATION_ICONS: Record<TenantNavigationIconKey, string> = {
  inicio: "🏠",
  dashboard: "📊",
  clientes: "👥",
  fornecedores: "🏢",
  produtos: "📦",
  transacoes: "⇄",
  vendas: "🛒",
  compras: "🧾",
  financeiro: "💳",
  ordensServico: "🛠️",
  usuarios: "👤",
  locatario: "🏬",
  assinatura: "💰",
  detalhes: "🔎",
  editar: "✏️",
  novo: "＋",
};

export function getTenantNavigationIcon(

): string {
  if (!iconKey) return "•";

}

    </span>

===== apps/web/src/shared/navigation/tenantRouteBreadcrumbs.ts =====

// apps/web/src/shared/navigation/tenantRouteBreadcrumbs.ts

import type { DomainBreadcrumbItem } from "./DomainBreadcrumbs";

type RouteMatch = {
  pattern: RegExp;
  items: DomainBreadcrumbItem[];
};

const ROUTES: RouteMatch[] = [
  { pattern: /^\/app\/?$/, items: [{ label: "Início", to: "/app", iconKey: "inicio" }] },
  {
    pattern: /^\/app\/clientes(?:\/.*)?$/,
    items: [
      { label: "Início", to: "/app", iconKey: "inicio" },
      { label: "Clientes", to: "/app/clientes", iconKey: "clientes" },
    ],
  },
  {
    pattern: /^\/app\/fornecedores(?:\/.*)?$/,
    items: [
      { label: "Início", to: "/app", iconKey: "inicio" },
      { label: "Fornecedores", to: "/app/fornecedores", iconKey: "fornecedores" },
    ],
  },
  {
    pattern: /^\/app\/produtos(?:\/.*)?$/,
    items: [
      { label: "Início", to: "/app", iconKey: "inicio" },
      { label: "Produtos", to: "/app/produtos", iconKey: "produtos" },
    ],
  },
  {
    pattern: /^\/app\/transacoes(?:\/.*)?$/,
    items: [
      { label: "Início", to: "/app", iconKey: "inicio" },
      { label: "Transações", to: "/app/transacoes", iconKey: "transacoes" },
    ],
  },
  {
    pattern: /^\/app\/financeiro(?:\/.*)?$/,
    items: [
      { label: "Início", to: "/app", iconKey: "inicio" },
      { label: "Financeiro", to: "/app/financeiro", iconKey: "financeiro" },
    ],
  },
  {
    pattern: /^\/app\/ordens-servico(?:\/.*)?$/,
    items: [
      { label: "Início", to: "/app", iconKey: "inicio" },
      { label: "Ordens de serviço", to: "/app/ordens-servico", iconKey: "ordensServico" },
    ],
  },
  {
    pattern: /^\/app\/vendas(?:\/.*)?$/,
    items: [
      { label: "Início", to: "/app", iconKey: "inicio" },
      { label: "Vendas", to: "/app/vendas", iconKey: "vendas" },
    ],
  },
  {
    pattern: /^\/app\/compras(?:\/.*)?$/,
    items: [
      { label: "Início", to: "/app", iconKey: "inicio" },
      { label: "Compras", to: "/app/compras", iconKey: "compras" },
    ],
  },
];

function appendContextItem(pathname: string, items: DomainBreadcrumbItem[]): DomainBreadcrumbItem[] {
  if (/\/novo\/?$/.test(pathname)) return [...items, { label: "Novo", iconKey: "novo" }];
  if (/\/editar\/?$/.test(pathname)) return [...items, { label: "Editar", iconKey: "editar" }];

  const isDetailsRoute = /^\/app\/[^/]+\/[^/]+/.test(pathname) && !/\/editar\/?$/.test(pathname);
  if (isDetailsRoute) return [...items, { label: "Detalhes", iconKey: "detalhes" }];

  return items;
}

export function getTenantBreadcrumbItems(pathname: string): DomainBreadcrumbItem[] {
  const route = ROUTES.find((item) => item.pattern.test(pathname));
  return appendContextItem(pathname, route?.items ?? [{ label: "Início", to: "/app", iconKey: "inicio" }]);
}

===== apps/web/src/shared/pwa/componentContracts.md =====

# Contratos de componentes PWA-ready

## Page

Pode:
- usar `useParams`;
- chamar API;
- navegar;
- controlar loading/error;
- montar callbacks.

Não deve:
- conter tabela grande inline;
- conter formulário complexo inline;
- duplicar lógica de filtro.

## Form

Pode:
- receber `value`;
- receber `onChange`;
- validar UX simples;
- chamar `onSubmit`.

Não deve:
- chamar API diretamente;
- ler rota;
- usar `localStorage` diretamente;
- navegar.

## Table

Pode:
- filtrar localmente;
- ordenar localmente;
- editar inline se receber callbacks;
- exibir estado vazio.

Não deve:
- chamar API;
- ler rota;
- depender de `PageGuard`.

## InlineForm

Pode:
- chamar API enquanto ainda estivermos online-first;
- devolver `onCreated(entity)`;
- completar DTO mínimo com os dados digitados;
- futuramente trocar API por fila offline.

Não deve:
- forçar reload global;
- navegar;
- depender da página atual.


===== apps/web/src/shared/pwa/createOfflineEntity.ts =====

// apps/web/src/shared/pwa/createOfflineEntity.ts

export function createOfflineId(prefix: string): string {
  if (typeof crypto !== "undefined" && "randomUUID" in crypto) {
    return `${prefix}_offline_${crypto.randomUUID()}`;
  }

  return `${prefix}_offline_${Date.now()}_${Math.random()
    .toString(16)
    .slice(2)}`;
}

export function isOfflineEntityId(id?: string | null): boolean {
  return Boolean(id?.includes("_offline_"));
}

export function markOfflineLabel(label: string): string {
  return label.includes("(pendente)") ? label : `${label} (pendente)`;
}

export function removeOfflineLabel(label: string): string {
  return label.replace(/\s+\(pendente\)$/i, "");
}


===== apps/web/src/shared/pwa/createScopedOfflineProcessor.ts =====

// apps/web/src/shared/pwa/createScopedOfflineProcessor.ts

import { clientesApi } from "../../features/tenant-admin/clientes/api/clientesApi";
import { comprasApi } from "../../features/tenant-admin/compras/api/comprasApi";
import { fornecedoresApi } from "../../features/tenant-admin/fornecedores/api/fornecedoresApi";
import { ordensServicoApi } from "../../features/tenant-admin/ordens-servico/api/ordensServicoApi";
import { produtosApi } from "../../features/tenant-admin/produtos/api/produtosApi";
import { transacoesApi } from "../../features/tenant-admin/transacoes/api/transacoesApi";
import { vendasApi } from "../../features/tenant-admin/vendas/api/vendasApi";
import { isOfflineEntityId } from "./createOfflineEntity";
import { createScopedOfflineIdMap } from "./scopedOfflineIdMap";
import type { PwaScope } from "./pwaScope";
import type { CriarOrdemServicoOfflinePayload } from "./offlineOsTypes";
import type {
  CriarCompraOfflinePayload,
  CriarVendaOfflinePayload,
} from "./offlineTransacaoTypes";
import type {
  AdicionarItemTransacaoOfflinePayload,
  AtualizarItemTransacaoOfflinePayload,
  RemoverItemTransacaoOfflinePayload,
} from "./offlineTransacaoItemTypes";
import type { OfflineEntityKind } from "./offlineIdMap";
import type { PwaQueueItem } from "./pwaSyncTypes";

function assertLocalId(item: PwaQueueItem): string {
  if (!item.localId) {
    throw new Error(`Item offline sem localId: ${item.type}`);
  }

  return item.localId;
}

class ScopedOfflineDependencyNotResolvedError extends Error {
  code = "DEPENDENCIA_OFFLINE_NAO_RESOLVIDA";

  constructor(kind: OfflineEntityKind, localId: string) {
    super(`Dependência offline ainda não resolvida: ${kind}:${localId}`);
  }
}

function resolveRequired(params: {
  idMap: ReturnType<typeof createScopedOfflineIdMap>;
  kind: OfflineEntityKind;
  id: string | null | undefined;
  label: string;
}): string {
  if (!params.id) {
    throw new Error(`ID obrigatório ausente: ${params.label}`);
  }

  const resolved = params.idMap.resolve(params.kind, params.id);

  if (!resolved) {
    throw new Error(`ID obrigatório não resolvido: ${params.label}`);
  }

  if (isOfflineEntityId(resolved)) {
    throw new ScopedOfflineDependencyNotResolvedError(params.kind, resolved);
  }

  return resolved;
}

function resolveOptional(params: {
  idMap: ReturnType<typeof createScopedOfflineIdMap>;
  kind: OfflineEntityKind;
  id: string | null | undefined;
}): string | null {
  if (!params.id) return null;

  const resolved = params.idMap.resolve(params.kind, params.id);

  if (resolved && isOfflineEntityId(resolved)) {
    throw new ScopedOfflineDependencyNotResolvedError(params.kind, resolved);
  }

  return resolved;
}

function resolveProdutoOuServicoId(
  idMap: ReturnType<typeof createScopedOfflineIdMap>,
  id: string | null | undefined,
): string | null {
  const comoServico = idMap.resolve("servico", id);
  const comoProduto = idMap.resolve("produto", comoServico);

  return comoProduto;
}

export function createScopedOfflineProcessor(scope: PwaScope) {
  const idMap = createScopedOfflineIdMap(scope);

  return async function scopedOfflineProcessor(item: PwaQueueItem) {
    switch (item.type) {
      case "clientes.criar": {
        const response = await clientesApi.criar(item.payload as never);

        idMap.register({
          kind: "cliente",
          localId: assertLocalId(item),
          remoteId: response.item.clienteId,
        });

        return;
      }

      case "fornecedores.criar": {
        const response = await fornecedoresApi.criar(item.payload as never);

        idMap.register({
          kind: "fornecedor",
          localId: assertLocalId(item),
          remoteId: response.item.fornecedorId,
        });

        return;
      }

      case "servicos.criar": {
        const response = await produtosApi.criar(item.payload as never);

        idMap.register({
          kind: "servico",
          localId: assertLocalId(item),
          remoteId: response.item.produtoId,
        });

        return;
      }

      case "ordensServico.criar": {
        const payload = item.payload as CriarOrdemServicoOfflinePayload;

        const clienteId = resolveOptional({
          idMap,
          kind: "cliente",
          id: payload.clienteId,
        });

        const servicoId = resolveOptional({
          idMap,
          kind: "servico",
          id: payload.servicoId,
        });

        const response = await ordensServicoApi.criar({
          ...payload,
          clienteId,
          servicoId,
        });

        if (item.localId) {
          idMap.register({
            kind: "ordemServico",
            localId: item.localId,
            remoteId: response.item.ordemServicoId,
          });
        }

        return;
      }

      case "vendas.criar": {
        const payload = item.payload as CriarVendaOfflinePayload;

        const clienteId = resolveOptional({
          idMap,
          kind: "cliente",
          id: payload.clienteId,
        });

        const response = await vendasApi.criar({
          ...payload,
          clienteId,
        });

        if (item.localId) {
          idMap.register({
            kind: "transacao",
            localId: item.localId,
            remoteId: response.item.transacaoId,
          });
        }

        return;
      }

      case "compras.criar": {
        const payload = item.payload as CriarCompraOfflinePayload;

        const fornecedorId = resolveOptional({
          idMap,
          kind: "fornecedor",
          id: payload.fornecedorId,
        });

        const response = await comprasApi.criar({
          ...payload,
          fornecedorId,
        });

        if (item.localId) {
          idMap.register({
            kind: "transacao",
            localId: item.localId,
            remoteId: response.item.transacaoId,
          });
        }

        return;
      }

      case "transacoes.itens.adicionar": {
        const payload = item.payload as AdicionarItemTransacaoOfflinePayload;

        const transacaoId = resolveRequired({
          idMap,
          kind: "transacao",
          id: payload.transacaoId,
          label: "transacaoId",
        });

        const produtoId = resolveProdutoOuServicoId(idMap, payload.produtoId);

        if (!produtoId || isOfflineEntityId(produtoId)) {
          throw new ScopedOfflineDependencyNotResolvedError(
            "servico",
            String(payload.produtoId),
          );
        }

        const response = await transacoesApi.adicionarItem(transacaoId, {
          produtoId,
          quantidade: payload.quantidade,
          valorUnitario: payload.valorUnitario ?? undefined,
          valorDesconto: payload.valorDesconto ?? undefined,
          descricao: payload.descricao ?? undefined,
        });

        if (item.localId && response.item?.itemTransacaoId) {
          idMap.register({
            kind: "produto",
            localId: item.localId,
            remoteId: response.item.itemTransacaoId,
          });
        }

        return;
      }

      case "transacoes.itens.atualizar": {
        const payload = item.payload as AtualizarItemTransacaoOfflinePayload;

        const transacaoId = resolveRequired({
          idMap,
          kind: "transacao",
          id: payload.transacaoId,
          label: "transacaoId",
        });

        const itemTransacaoId = resolveRequired({
          idMap,
          kind: "produto",
          id: payload.itemTransacaoId,
          label: "itemTransacaoId",
        });

        await transacoesApi.atualizarItem(transacaoId, itemTransacaoId, {
          quantidade: payload.quantidade,
          valorUnitario: payload.valorUnitario,
          valorDesconto: payload.valorDesconto,
          descricao: payload.descricao,
        });

        return;
      }

      case "transacoes.itens.remover": {
        const payload = item.payload as RemoverItemTransacaoOfflinePayload;

        const transacaoId = resolveRequired({
          idMap,
          kind: "transacao",
          id: payload.transacaoId,
          label: "transacaoId",
        });

        const itemTransacaoId = resolveRequired({
          idMap,
          kind: "produto",
          id: payload.itemTransacaoId,
          label: "itemTransacaoId",
        });

        await transacoesApi.removerItem(transacaoId, itemTransacaoId);
        return;
      }

      default:
        throw new Error(`Tipo de mutação offline não suportado: ${item.type}`);
    }
  };
}

===== apps/web/src/shared/pwa/offlineCreateProcessor.patch.md =====

# Patch recomendado no offlineCreateProcessor

Troque resoluções manuais por:

```ts
import {
  resolveOptionalOfflineDependency,
  resolveRequiredOfflineDependency,
} from "./offlineDependencies";
```

Exemplos:

## Venda

```ts
const clienteId = resolveOptionalOfflineDependency(
  "cliente",
  payload.clienteId,
);
```

## Compra

```ts
const fornecedorId = resolveOptionalOfflineDependency(
  "fornecedor",
  payload.fornecedorId,
);
```

## Item de transação

```ts
const transacaoId = resolveRequiredOfflineDependency(
  "transacao",
  payload.transacaoId,
  "transacaoId",
);

const produtoId = resolveRequiredOfflineDependency(
  "servico",
  payload.produtoId,
  "produtoId",
);
```

Assim, quando uma dependência ainda não tiver ID real, a fila marca como erro de dependência.


===== apps/web/src/shared/pwa/offlineCreateProcessor.ts =====

// apps/web/src/shared/pwa/offlineCreateProcessor.ts

import { clientesApi } from "../../features/tenant-admin/clientes/api/clientesApi";
import { comprasApi } from "../../features/tenant-admin/compras/api/comprasApi";
import { fornecedoresApi } from "../../features/tenant-admin/fornecedores/api/fornecedoresApi";
import { ordensServicoApi } from "../../features/tenant-admin/ordens-servico/api/ordensServicoApi";
import { produtosApi } from "../../features/tenant-admin/produtos/api/produtosApi";
import { transacoesApi } from "../../features/tenant-admin/transacoes/api/transacoesApi";
import { vendasApi } from "../../features/tenant-admin/vendas/api/vendasApi";
import { offlineIdMap, resolveOfflineId } from "./offlineIdMap";
import type { CriarOrdemServicoOfflinePayload } from "./offlineOsTypes";
import type {
  CriarCompraOfflinePayload,
  CriarVendaOfflinePayload,
} from "./offlineTransacaoTypes";
import type {
  AdicionarItemTransacaoOfflinePayload,
  AtualizarItemTransacaoOfflinePayload,
  RemoverItemTransacaoOfflinePayload,
} from "./offlineTransacaoItemTypes";
import type { PwaQueueItem } from "./pwaSyncTypes";

function assertLocalId(item: PwaQueueItem): string {
  if (!item.localId) {
    throw new Error(`Item offline sem localId: ${item.type}`);
  }

  return item.localId;
}

function resolveProdutoOuServicoId(id: string | null | undefined): string | null {
  const comoServico = resolveOfflineId("servico", id);
  const comoProduto = resolveOfflineId("produto", comoServico);

  return comoProduto;
}

function assertResolvedId(id: string | null, label: string): string {
  if (!id) {
    throw new Error(`ID obrigatório não resolvido: ${label}`);
  }

  return id;
}

export async function offlineCreateProcessor(item: PwaQueueItem) {
  switch (item.type) {
    case "clientes.criar": {
      const response = await clientesApi.criar(item.payload as never);

      offlineIdMap.register({
        kind: "cliente",
        localId: assertLocalId(item),
        remoteId: response.item.clienteId,
      });

      return;
    }

    case "fornecedores.criar": {
      const response = await fornecedoresApi.criar(item.payload as never);

      offlineIdMap.register({
        kind: "fornecedor",
        localId: assertLocalId(item),
        remoteId: response.item.fornecedorId,
      });

      return;
    }

    case "servicos.criar": {
      const response = await produtosApi.criar(item.payload as never);

      offlineIdMap.register({
        kind: "servico",
        localId: assertLocalId(item),
        remoteId: response.item.produtoId,
      });

      return;
    }

    case "ordensServico.criar": {
      const payload = item.payload as CriarOrdemServicoOfflinePayload;

      const clienteId = resolveOfflineId("cliente", payload.clienteId);
      const servicoId = resolveOfflineId("servico", payload.servicoId);

      const response = await ordensServicoApi.criar({
        ...payload,
        clienteId,
        servicoId,
      });

      if (item.localId) {
        offlineIdMap.register({
          kind: "ordemServico",
          localId: item.localId,
          remoteId: response.item.ordemServicoId,
        });
      }

      return;
    }

    case "vendas.criar": {
      const payload = item.payload as CriarVendaOfflinePayload;
      const clienteId = resolveOfflineId("cliente", payload.clienteId);

      const response = await vendasApi.criar({
        ...payload,
        clienteId,
      });

      if (item.localId) {
        offlineIdMap.register({
          kind: "transacao",
          localId: item.localId,
          remoteId: response.item.transacaoId,
        });
      }

      return;
    }

    case "compras.criar": {
      const payload = item.payload as CriarCompraOfflinePayload;
      const fornecedorId = resolveOfflineId("fornecedor", payload.fornecedorId);

      const response = await comprasApi.criar({
        ...payload,
        fornecedorId,
      });

      if (item.localId) {
        offlineIdMap.register({
          kind: "transacao",
          localId: item.localId,
          remoteId: response.item.transacaoId,
        });
      }

      return;
    }

    case "transacoes.itens.adicionar": {
      const payload = item.payload as AdicionarItemTransacaoOfflinePayload;

      const transacaoId = assertResolvedId(
        resolveOfflineId("transacao", payload.transacaoId),
        "transacaoId",
      );

      const produtoId = assertResolvedId(
        resolveProdutoOuServicoId(payload.produtoId),
        "produtoId",
      );

      const response = await transacoesApi.adicionarItem(transacaoId, {
        produtoId,
        quantidade: payload.quantidade,
        valorUnitario: payload.valorUnitario ?? undefined,
        valorDesconto: payload.valorDesconto ?? undefined,
        descricao: payload.descricao ?? undefined,
      });

      if (item.localId && response.item?.itemTransacaoId) {
        offlineIdMap.register({
          kind: "produto",
          localId: item.localId,
          remoteId: response.item.itemTransacaoId,
        });
      }

      return;
    }

    case "transacoes.itens.atualizar": {
      const payload = item.payload as AtualizarItemTransacaoOfflinePayload;

      const transacaoId = assertResolvedId(
        resolveOfflineId("transacao", payload.transacaoId),
        "transacaoId",
      );

      const itemTransacaoId = assertResolvedId(
        resolveOfflineId("produto", payload.itemTransacaoId),
        "itemTransacaoId",
      );

      await transacoesApi.atualizarItem(transacaoId, itemTransacaoId, {
        quantidade: payload.quantidade,
        valorUnitario: payload.valorUnitario,
        valorDesconto: payload.valorDesconto,
        descricao: payload.descricao,
      });

      return;
    }

    case "transacoes.itens.remover": {
      const payload = item.payload as RemoverItemTransacaoOfflinePayload;

      const transacaoId = assertResolvedId(
        resolveOfflineId("transacao", payload.transacaoId),
        "transacaoId",
      );

      const itemTransacaoId = assertResolvedId(
        resolveOfflineId("produto", payload.itemTransacaoId),
        "itemTransacaoId",
      );

      await transacoesApi.removerItem(transacaoId, itemTransacaoId);

      return;
    }

    default:
      throw new Error(`Tipo de mutação offline não suportado: ${item.type}`);
  }
}


===== apps/web/src/shared/pwa/offlineDependencies.ts =====

// apps/web/src/shared/pwa/offlineDependencies.ts

import { isOfflineEntityId } from "./createOfflineEntity";
import { resolveOfflineId, type OfflineEntityKind } from "./offlineIdMap";

export class OfflineDependencyNotResolvedError extends Error {
  code = "DEPENDENCIA_OFFLINE_NAO_RESOLVIDA";

  constructor(
    public readonly kind: OfflineEntityKind,
    public readonly localId: string,
  ) {
    super(`Dependência offline ainda não resolvida: ${kind}:${localId}`);
  }
}

export function resolveRequiredOfflineDependency(
  kind: OfflineEntityKind,
  id: string | null | undefined,
  label: string,
): string {
  if (!id) {
    throw new Error(`ID obrigatório ausente: ${label}`);
  }

  const resolved = resolveOfflineId(kind, id);

  if (!resolved) {
    throw new Error(`ID obrigatório não resolvido: ${label}`);
  }

  if (isOfflineEntityId(resolved)) {
    throw new OfflineDependencyNotResolvedError(kind, resolved);
  }

  return resolved;
}

export function resolveOptionalOfflineDependency(
  kind: OfflineEntityKind,
  id: string | null | undefined,
): string | null {
  if (!id) return null;

  const resolved = resolveOfflineId(kind, id);

  if (resolved && isOfflineEntityId(resolved)) {
    throw new OfflineDependencyNotResolvedError(kind, resolved);
  }

  return resolved;
}


===== apps/web/src/shared/pwa/offlineIdMap.ts =====

// apps/web/src/shared/pwa/offlineIdMap.ts

const ID_MAP_KEY = "corekit:pwa:id-map";

export type OfflineEntityKind =
  | "cliente"
  | "fornecedor"
  | "produto"
  | "servico"
  | "transacao"
  | "ordemServico";

export type OfflineIdMapEntry = {
  kind: OfflineEntityKind;
  localId: string;
  remoteId: string;
  syncedAt: string;
};

function safeParse<T>(raw: string | null, fallback: T): T {
  if (!raw) return fallback;

  try {
    return JSON.parse(raw) as T;
  } catch {
    return fallback;
  }
}

function readMap(): OfflineIdMapEntry[] {
  if (typeof localStorage === "undefined") return [];

  return safeParse<OfflineIdMapEntry[]>(
    localStorage.getItem(ID_MAP_KEY),
    [],
  );
}

function writeMap(entries: OfflineIdMapEntry[]) {
  if (typeof localStorage === "undefined") return;

  localStorage.setItem(ID_MAP_KEY, JSON.stringify(entries));
}

export const offlineIdMap = {
  list(): OfflineIdMapEntry[] {
    return readMap();
  },

  register(params: {
    kind: OfflineEntityKind;
    localId: string;
    remoteId: string;
  }) {
    const current = readMap();

    const next: OfflineIdMapEntry[] = [
      {
        kind: params.kind,
        localId: params.localId,
        remoteId: params.remoteId,
        syncedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      },
      ...current.filter(
        (entry) =>
          !(
            entry.kind === params.kind &&
            entry.localId === params.localId
          ),
      ),
    ];

    writeMap(next);
  },

  resolve(
    kind: OfflineEntityKind,
    id: string | null | undefined,
  ): string | null {
    if (!id) return null;

    const found = readMap().find(
      (entry) => entry.kind === kind && entry.localId === id,
    );

    return found?.remoteId ?? id;
  },

  remove(kind: OfflineEntityKind, localId: string) {
    writeMap(
      readMap().filter(
        (entry) => !(entry.kind === kind && entry.localId === localId),
      ),
    );
  },

  clear() {
    if (typeof localStorage === "undefined") return;
    localStorage.removeItem(ID_MAP_KEY);
  },
};

export function resolveOfflineId(
  kind: OfflineEntityKind,
  id: string | null | undefined,
): string | null {
  return offlineIdMap.resolve(kind, id);
}


===== apps/web/src/shared/pwa/offlineInlineCreate.README.md =====

# Criação offline nos cadastros inline

## Arquivos

- `shared/pwa/createOfflineEntity.ts`
- `shared/pwa/offlineCreateProcessor.ts`
- `shared/pwa/OfflineSyncBootstrap.tsx`
- `clientes/components/InlineClienteForm.tsx`
- `fornecedores/components/InlineFornecedorForm.tsx`
- `produtos/components/InlineServicoForm.tsx`

## Como ativar a sincronização

No layout autenticado, por exemplo `TenantAdminLayout` ou `TenantAdminShell`, adicione:

```tsx
import { OfflineSyncBootstrap } from "../../../shared/pwa/OfflineSyncBootstrap";

export function TenantAdminLayout() {
  return (
    <>
      <OfflineSyncBootstrap />
      {/* resto do layout */}
    </>
  );
}
```

## Comportamento

Offline:

- o cadastro é adicionado ao select imediatamente;
- o nome aparece com `(pendente)`;
- a mutação entra em `corekit:pwa:queue`.

Online:

- o cadastro usa a API normalmente;
- se houver itens pendentes, `OfflineSyncBootstrap` tenta sincronizar.

## Limitação conhecida

O ID temporário não é reconciliado com o ID real do backend nesta etapa.
Para OS/venda vinculada a cliente offline, será necessário um mapa de IDs temporários → IDs reais antes de sincronizar transações complexas.

===== apps/web/src/shared/pwa/OfflineQueueDetails.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/shared/pwa/OfflineQueueDetails.tsx

import type { PwaQueueItem } from "./pwaSyncTypes";

type OfflineQueueDetailsProps = {
  item: PwaQueueItem | null;
  onClose: () => void;
};

function formatJson(value: unknown): string {
  try {
    return JSON.stringify(value, null, 2);
  } catch {
    return String(value);
  }
}

export function OfflineQueueDetails({
  item,
  onClose,
}: OfflineQueueDetailsProps) {
  if (!item) return null;

  return (
    <section className="ck-register-filter">
      <div className="ck-register-filter-grid">
        <div>
          <strong>Detalhes da pendência</strong>
          <p className="ck-entity-description">
            {item.type} • {item.status}
            {item.lastErrorKind ? ` • ${item.lastErrorKind}` : ""}
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          className="ck-register-button"
          type="button"
          onClick={onClose}
        >
          Fechar detalhes
        </button>
      </div>

      {item.lastError ? (
        <div className="ck-register-alert">
          {item.lastError}
        </div>
      ) : null}

      <pre
        style={{
          whiteSpace: "pre-wrap",
          overflowX: "auto",
        }}
      >
        {formatJson({
          id: item.id,
          localId: item.localId,
          type: item.type,
          status: item.status,
          attempts: item.attempts,
          createdAt: item.createdAt,
          updatedAt: item.updatedAt,
          payload: item.payload,
          lastError: item.lastError,
          lastErrorKind: item.lastErrorKind,
          lastErrorDetails: item.lastErrorDetails,
        })}
      </pre>
    </section>
  );
}


===== apps/web/src/shared/pwa/OfflineQueuePanel.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/shared/pwa/OfflineQueuePanel.tsx

import { useState } from "react";
import { useOfflineQueueView } from "./useOfflineQueueView";
import { OfflineQueueTable } from "./OfflineQueueTable";
import { OfflineQueueDetails } from "./OfflineQueueDetails";
import type { PwaQueueItem } from "./pwaSyncTypes";

type OfflineQueuePanelProps = {
  compact?: boolean;
};

export function OfflineQueuePanel({ compact = false }: OfflineQueuePanelProps) {
  const queue = useOfflineQueueView();
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<PwaQueueItem | null>(null);

  if (compact && queue.totalCount === 0) {
    return null;
  }

  return (
    <section className="ck-register-page">
      <section className="ck-register-hero">
        <div className="ck-register-hero-inner">
          <div>
            <div className="ck-register-kicker">PWA</div>
            <h1 className="ck-register-title">Sincronização</h1>
            <p className="ck-register-description">
              Acompanhe operações offline pendentes, falhas e bloqueios.
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="ck-register-actions">
            <button
              className="ck-register-action-primary"
              type="button"
              disabled={!queue.isOnline || queue.isSyncing || queue.totalCount === 0}
              onClick={() => void queue.sync()}
            >
              {queue.isSyncing ? "Sincronizando..." : "Sincronizar agora"}
            </button>

            <button
              className="ck-register-button"
              type="button"
              onClick={queue.refresh}
            >
              Atualizar
            </button>

            <button
              className="ck-register-button"
              type="button"
              disabled={queue.totalCount === 0 || queue.isSyncing}
              onClick={queue.clear}
            >
              Limpar fila
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="ck-register-stats">
        <article className="ck-register-stat">
          <div className="ck-register-stat-label">Pendentes</div>
          <div className="ck-register-stat-value">{queue.pendingCount}</div>
        </article>

        <article className="ck-register-stat">
          <div className="ck-register-stat-label">Falhas</div>
          <div className="ck-register-stat-value">{queue.failedCount}</div>
        </article>

        <article className="ck-register-stat">
          <div className="ck-register-stat-label">Bloqueadas</div>
          <div className="ck-register-stat-value">{queue.blockedCount}</div>
        </article>

        <article className="ck-register-stat">
          <div className="ck-register-stat-label">Sincronizando</div>
          <div className="ck-register-stat-value">{queue.syncingCount}</div>
        </article>
      </section>

      {queue.isOffline ? (
        <div className="ck-register-alert">
          Você está offline. A sincronização será retomada quando a conexão voltar.
        </div>
      ) : null}

      <OfflineQueueDetails
        item={selected}
        onClose={() => setSelected(null)}
      />

      <OfflineQueueTable
        items={queue.items}
        isOnline={queue.isOnline}
        isSyncing={queue.isSyncing}
        onDetails={setSelected}
        onRetry={queue.retry}
        onBlock={queue.block}
        onUnblock={queue.unblock}
        onRemove={queue.remove}
      />
    </section>
  );
}


===== apps/web/src/shared/pwa/OfflineQueueTable.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/shared/pwa/OfflineQueueTable.tsx

import type { PwaQueueItem } from "./pwaSyncTypes";

type OfflineQueueTableProps = {
  items: PwaQueueItem[];
  isOnline: boolean;
  isSyncing: boolean;
  onDetails: (item: PwaQueueItem) => void;
  onRetry: (id: string) => void;
  onBlock: (id: string) => void;
  onUnblock: (id: string) => void;
  onRemove: (id: string) => void;
};

function formatDate(value: string): string {
  try {
    return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", {
      dateStyle: "short",
      timeStyle: "short",
    }).format(new Date(value));
  } catch {
    return value;
  }
}

function payloadResumo(item: PwaQueueItem): string {
  const payload = item.payload as Record<string, unknown>;

  const candidates = [
    payload?.nome,
    payload?.titulo,
    payload?.observacao,
    payload?.descricao,
    payload?.clienteId,
    payload?.fornecedorId,
    payload?.produtoId,
  ].filter(Boolean);

  return candidates.length > 0 ? String(candidates[0]) : "Sem resumo";
}

export function OfflineQueueTable({
  items,
  isOnline,
  isSyncing,
  onDetails,
  onRetry,
  onBlock,
  onUnblock,
  onRemove,
}: OfflineQueueTableProps) {
  if (items.length === 0) {
    return (
      <p className="ck-entity-description">
        Nenhuma pendência offline no momento.
      </p>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="ck-register-table-panel">
      <table className="ck-register-table">
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>Operação</th>
            <th>Resumo</th>
            <th>Status</th>
            <th>Erro</th>
            <th>Tentativas</th>
            <th>Criado em</th>
            <th>Ações</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>

        <tbody>
          {items.map((item) => (
            <tr key={item.id}>
              <td>{item.type}</td>
              <td>{payloadResumo(item)}</td>
              <td>{item.status}</td>
              <td>{item.lastErrorKind ?? "—"}</td>
              <td>{item.attempts}</td>
              <td>{formatDate(item.createdAt)}</td>
              <td>
                <div className="ck-entity-actions-row">
                  <button
                    className="ck-register-button"
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => onDetails(item)}
                  >
                    Detalhes
                  </button>

                  {item.status === "failed" || item.status === "blocked" ? (
                    <button
                      className="ck-register-button"
                      type="button"
                      disabled={!isOnline || isSyncing}
                      onClick={() => onRetry(item.id)}
                    >
                      Tentar novamente
                    </button>
                  ) : null}

                  {item.status !== "blocked" ? (
                    <button
                      className="ck-register-button"
                      type="button"
                      disabled={item.status === "syncing"}
                      onClick={() => onBlock(item.id)}
                    >
                      Bloquear
                    </button>
                  ) : (
                    <button
                      className="ck-register-button"
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => onUnblock(item.id)}
                    >
                      Desbloquear
                    </button>
                  )}

                  <button
                    className="ck-register-button"
                    type="button"
                    disabled={item.status === "syncing"}
                    onClick={() => onRemove(item.id)}
                  >
                    Remover
                  </button>
                </div>
              </td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
}


===== apps/web/src/shared/pwa/offlineQueue.ts =====

// apps/web/src/shared/pwa/offlineQueue.ts

import { offlineStorage } from "./offlineStorage";
import { classifySyncError } from "./syncErrorClassifier";
import type { PwaQueueItem, PwaQueueProcessor } from "./pwaSyncTypes";

function safeParseQueue(raw: string | null): PwaQueueItem[] {
  if (!raw) return [];

  try {
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    return Array.isArray(parsed) ? (parsed as PwaQueueItem[]) : [];
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

function createId(): string {
  if (typeof crypto !== "undefined" && "randomUUID" in crypto) {
    return crypto.randomUUID();
  }

  return `${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(16).slice(2)}`;
}

function nowIso(): string {
  return new Date().toISOString();
}

export const offlineQueue = {
  list(): PwaQueueItem[] {
    return safeParseQueue(offlineStorage.getQueueRaw());
  },

  save(items: PwaQueueItem[]): void {
    offlineStorage.setQueueRaw(JSON.stringify(items));
  },

  enqueue<TPayload>(params: {
    type: string;
    payload: TPayload;
    localId?: string | null;
  }): PwaQueueItem<TPayload> {
    const current = this.list();

    const item: PwaQueueItem<TPayload> = {
      id: createId(),
      type: params.type,
      payload: params.payload,
      localId: params.localId ?? null,
      status: "pending",
      createdAt: nowIso(),
      updatedAt: nowIso(),
      attempts: 0,
      lastError: null,
      lastErrorKind: null,
      lastErrorDetails: null,
    };

    this.save([item, ...current]);

    return item;
  },

  remove(id: string): void {
    this.save(this.list().filter((item) => item.id !== id));
  },

  update(id: string, patch: Partial<PwaQueueItem>): void {
    this.save(
      this.list().map((item) =>
        item.id === id
          ? {
              ...item,
              ...patch,
              updatedAt: nowIso(),
            }
          : item,
      ),
    );
  },

  block(id: string, reason?: string): void {
    this.update(id, {
      status: "blocked",
      lastError: reason ?? "Pendência bloqueada manualmente.",
      lastErrorKind: "unknown",
    });
  },

  unblock(id: string): void {
    this.update(id, {
      status: "pending",
      lastError: null,
      lastErrorKind: null,
      lastErrorDetails: null,
    });
  },

  async process(processor: PwaQueueProcessor): Promise<void> {
    const items = this.list().filter(
      (item) => item.status === "pending" || item.status === "failed",
    );

    for (const item of items.reverse()) {
      this.update(item.id, {
        status: "syncing",
        attempts: item.attempts + 1,
        lastError: null,
        lastErrorKind: null,
        lastErrorDetails: null,
      });

      try {
        await processor(item);
        this.remove(item.id);
      } catch (cause) {
        const classified = classifySyncError(cause);

        this.update(item.id, {
          status: classified.retryable ? "failed" : "blocked",
          lastError: classified.message,
          lastErrorKind: classified.kind,
          lastErrorDetails: classified.details,
        });
      }
    }
  },

  clear(): void {
    offlineStorage.removeQueue();
  },
};


===== apps/web/src/shared/pwa/offlineStorage.ts =====

// apps/web/src/shared/pwa/offlineStorage.ts

import type { PwaCacheRecord } from "./pwaSyncTypes";

const CACHE_PREFIX = "corekit:pwa:cache:";
const QUEUE_KEY = "corekit:pwa:queue";

function safeParse<T>(raw: string | null, fallback: T): T {
  if (!raw) return fallback;

  try {
    return JSON.parse(raw) as T;
  } catch {
    return fallback;
  }
}

function cacheStorageKey(key: string): string {
  return `${CACHE_PREFIX}${key}`;
}

export const offlineStorage = {
  getCache<TData>(key: string): PwaCacheRecord<TData> | null {
    if (typeof localStorage === "undefined") return null;

    return safeParse<PwaCacheRecord<TData> | null>(
      localStorage.getItem(cacheStorageKey(key)),
      null,
    );
  },

  setCache<TData>(key: string, data: TData): PwaCacheRecord<TData> {
    const record: PwaCacheRecord<TData> = {
      key,
      data,
      updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      version: 1,
    };

    if (typeof localStorage !== "undefined") {
      localStorage.setItem(cacheStorageKey(key), JSON.stringify(record));
    }

    return record;
  },

  removeCache(key: string): void {
    if (typeof localStorage === "undefined") return;

    localStorage.removeItem(cacheStorageKey(key));
  },

  clearCache(): void {
    if (typeof localStorage === "undefined") return;

    const keys = Array.from({ length: localStorage.length })
      .map((_, index) => localStorage.key(index))
      .filter((key): key is string => Boolean(key))
      .filter((key) => key.startsWith(CACHE_PREFIX));

    for (const key of keys) {
      localStorage.removeItem(key);
    }
  },

  getQueueRaw(): string | null {
    if (typeof localStorage === "undefined") return null;
    return localStorage.getItem(QUEUE_KEY);
  },

  setQueueRaw(raw: string): void {
    if (typeof localStorage === "undefined") return;
    localStorage.setItem(QUEUE_KEY, raw);
  },

  removeQueue(): void {
    if (typeof localStorage === "undefined") return;
    localStorage.removeItem(QUEUE_KEY);
  },
};

===== apps/web/src/shared/pwa/OfflineSyncStatus.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/shared/pwa/OfflineSyncStatus.tsx

import { useOfflineMutationQueue } from "./useOfflineMutationQueue";
import { offlineCreateProcessor } from "./offlineCreateProcessor";

export function OfflineSyncStatus() {
  const queue = useOfflineMutationQueue(offlineCreateProcessor);

  if (queue.pendingCount === 0 && !queue.isOffline && !queue.isSyncing) {
    return null;
  }

  return (
    <div className="ck-register-alert">
      {queue.isOffline ? "Você está offline. " : null}
      {queue.pendingCount > 0
        ? `${queue.pendingCount} item(ns) aguardando sincronização. `
        : null}
      {queue.isSyncing ? "Sincronizando pendências..." : null}

      {queue.isOnline && queue.pendingCount > 0 ? (
        <button type="button" onClick={() => void queue.sync()}>
          Sincronizar agora
        </button>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}

===== apps/web/src/shared/pwa/pwaCacheInvalidation.ts =====

// apps/web/src/shared/pwa/pwaCacheInvalidation.ts

import { offlineStorage } from "./offlineStorage";
import { buildPwaCacheKey, type PwaScope } from "./pwaScope";
import { emitPwaCacheInvalidated } from "./pwaSyncEvents";
import type { PwaQueueItem } from "./pwaSyncTypes";

export const PWA_RESOURCE_CACHE_KEYS = {
  clientes: "clientes:list",
  fornecedores: "fornecedores:list",
  produtos: "produtos:list",
  servicos: "servicos:list",
  ordensServico: "ordens-servico:list",
  transacoes: "transacoes:list",
  financeiro: "financeiro:resumo",
} as const;

export function getCacheKeysAffectedByQueueItem(item: PwaQueueItem): string[] {
  switch (item.type) {
    case "clientes.criar":
      return [PWA_RESOURCE_CACHE_KEYS.clientes];

    case "fornecedores.criar":
      return [PWA_RESOURCE_CACHE_KEYS.fornecedores];

    case "servicos.criar":
      return [
        PWA_RESOURCE_CACHE_KEYS.produtos,
        PWA_RESOURCE_CACHE_KEYS.servicos,
      ];

    case "ordensServico.criar":
      return [
        PWA_RESOURCE_CACHE_KEYS.ordensServico,
        PWA_RESOURCE_CACHE_KEYS.transacoes,
      ];

    case "vendas.criar":
    case "compras.criar":
      return [
        PWA_RESOURCE_CACHE_KEYS.transacoes,
        PWA_RESOURCE_CACHE_KEYS.financeiro,
      ];

    case "transacoes.itens.adicionar":
    case "transacoes.itens.atualizar":
    case "transacoes.itens.remover":
      return [
        PWA_RESOURCE_CACHE_KEYS.transacoes,
        PWA_RESOURCE_CACHE_KEYS.produtos,
        PWA_RESOURCE_CACHE_KEYS.financeiro,
      ];

    default:
      return [];
  }
}

export function invalidateScopedCacheKeys(params: {
  scope: PwaScope;
  keys: string[];
  reason: string;
}) {
  const scopedKeys = params.keys.map((key) =>
    buildPwaCacheKey(params.scope, key),
  );

  for (const key of scopedKeys) {
    offlineStorage.removeCache(key);
  }

  emitPwaCacheInvalidated({
    keys: params.keys,
    reason: params.reason,
  });
}

export function invalidateCachesForSyncedItem(params: {
  scope: PwaScope;
  item: PwaQueueItem;
}) {
  const keys = getCacheKeysAffectedByQueueItem(params.item);

  if (keys.length === 0) return;

  invalidateScopedCacheKeys({
    scope: params.scope,
    keys,
    reason: params.item.type,
  });
}

===== apps/web/src/shared/pwa/PwaMaintenancePanel.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/shared/pwa/PwaMaintenancePanel.tsx

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import {
  clearAllPwaData,
  clearPwaLocalData,
  clearServiceWorkerCaches,
  getPwaCacheNames,
} from "./pwaMaintenance";

export function PwaMaintenancePanel() {
  const [cacheNames, setCacheNames] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  async function refreshCaches() {
    setCacheNames(await getPwaCacheNames());
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    void refreshCaches();
  }, []);

  async function handleClearCaches() {
    await clearServiceWorkerCaches();
    await refreshCaches();
    setMessage("Caches do Service Worker limpos.");
  }

  function handleClearLocalData() {
    clearPwaLocalData();
    setMessage("Dados locais PWA limpos.");
  }

  async function handleClearAll() {
    await clearAllPwaData();
    await refreshCaches();
    setMessage("Todos os dados PWA foram limpos.");
  }

  return (
    <section className="ck-register-filter">
      <div className="ck-register-filter-grid">
        <div>
          <strong>Manutenção PWA</strong>
          <p className="ck-entity-description">
            Use estas ações apenas para diagnóstico, suporte ou limpeza de estado local.
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {message ? <div className="ck-register-alert">{message}</div> : null}

      <div className="ck-entity-actions-row">
        <button
          className="ck-register-button"
          type="button"
          onClick={() => void handleClearCaches()}
        >
          Limpar caches do app
        </button>

        <button
          className="ck-register-button"
          type="button"
          onClick={handleClearLocalData}
        >
          Limpar dados locais PWA
        </button>

        <button
          className="ck-register-button"
          type="button"
          onClick={() => void handleClearAll()}
        >
          Limpar tudo
        </button>

        <button
          className="ck-register-button"
          type="button"
          onClick={() => void refreshCaches()}
        >
          Atualizar lista
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="ck-register-table-panel">
        <table className="ck-register-table">
          <thead>
            <tr>
              <th>Caches ativos</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>

          <tbody>
            {cacheNames.length === 0 ? (
              <tr>
                <td>Nenhum cache encontrado.</td>
              </tr>
            ) : (
              cacheNames.map((name) => (
                <tr key={name}>
                  <td>{name}</td>
                </tr>
              ))
            )}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}


===== apps/web/src/shared/pwa/pwaMaintenance.ts =====

// apps/web/src/shared/pwa/pwaMaintenance.ts

import { offlineStorage } from "./offlineStorage";
import { offlineIdMap } from "./offlineIdMap";
import { offlineQueue } from "./offlineQueue";

export async function clearServiceWorkerCaches() {
  if (!("caches" in window)) return;

  const keys = await caches.keys();

  await Promise.all(
    keys
      .filter((key) => key.startsWith("corekit-"))
      .map((key) => caches.delete(key)),
  );

  const registration = await navigator.serviceWorker?.getRegistration();

  registration?.active?.postMessage({
    type: "CLEAR_PWA_CACHES",
  });
}

export function clearPwaLocalData() {
  offlineStorage.clearCache();
  offlineQueue.clear();
  offlineIdMap.clear();
}

export async function clearAllPwaData() {
  clearPwaLocalData();
  await clearServiceWorkerCaches();
}

export async function getPwaCacheNames(): Promise<string[]> {
  if (!("caches" in window)) return [];

  return caches.keys();
}

// apps/web/src/shared/pwa/PwaPendingBadge.tsx

import { getPwaPendingLabel, isPwaPendingId } from "./pwaPendingHelpers";

type PwaPendingBadgeProps = {

  label?: string;
};

export function PwaPendingBadge({ id, label }: PwaPendingBadgeProps) {
  if (!isPwaPendingId(id)) return null;

  return (
    <span
      className="ck-pwa-badge ck-pwa-badge--pending"
      title="Este registro foi criado offline e ainda não foi sincronizado."
    >
      {label ?? getPwaPendingLabel(id)}
    </span>
  );
}


===== apps/web/src/shared/pwa/pwaPendingHelpers.ts =====

// apps/web/src/shared/pwa/pwaPendingHelpers.ts

import { isOfflineEntityId } from "./createOfflineEntity";
import type { PwaQueueItem } from "./pwaSyncTypes";

export function isPwaPendingId(id?: string | null): boolean {
  return isOfflineEntityId(id);
}

export function getPwaPendingLabel(id?: string | null): string | null {
  if (!isPwaPendingId(id)) return null;

  if (id?.startsWith("cliente_offline_")) return "Cliente pendente";
  if (id?.startsWith("fornecedor_offline_")) return "Fornecedor pendente";
  if (id?.startsWith("servico_offline_")) return "Serviço pendente";
  if (id?.startsWith("produto_offline_")) return "Produto pendente";
  if (id?.startsWith("transacao_offline_")) return "Transação pendente";
  if (id?.startsWith("ordemServico_offline_")) return "OS pendente";
  if (id?.startsWith("itemTransacao_offline_")) return "Item pendente";

  return "Pendente";
}

export function getPwaQueueStatusLabel(status: PwaQueueItem["status"]): string {
  switch (status) {
    case "pending":
      return "Pendente";
    case "syncing":
      return "Sincronizando";
    case "failed":
      return "Falhou";
    case "blocked":
      return "Bloqueado";
    case "synced":
      return "Sincronizado";
    default:
      return status;
  }
}

export function getPwaQueueItemTitle(item: PwaQueueItem): string {
  const payload = item.payload as Record<string, unknown>;

  return String(
    payload.nome ??
      payload.titulo ??
      payload.descricao ??
      payload.observacao ??
      item.localId ??
      item.id,
  );
}


===== apps/web/src/shared/pwa/PwaPendingPanel.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/shared/pwa/PwaPendingPanel.tsx

import { getPwaQueueItemTitle } from "./pwaPendingHelpers";
import { PwaSyncBadge } from "./PwaSyncBadge";
import { usePendingQueueItems } from "./usePendingQueueItems";

type PwaPendingPanelProps = {
  title?: string;
  types?: string[];
  localId?: string | null;
};

export function PwaPendingPanel({
  title = "Pendências desta tela",
  types,
  localId,
}: PwaPendingPanelProps) {
  const queue = usePendingQueueItems({ types, localId });

  if (queue.count === 0) return null;

  return (
    <section className="ck-register-filter">
      <strong>{title}</strong>

      <div className="ck-register-table-panel">
        <table className="ck-register-table">
          <thead>
            <tr>
              <th>Operação</th>
              <th>Registro</th>
              <th>Status</th>
              <th>Erro</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>

          <tbody>
            {queue.items.map((item) => (
              <tr key={item.id}>
                <td>{item.type}</td>
                <td>{getPwaQueueItemTitle(item)}</td>
                <td>
                  <PwaSyncBadge status={item.status} />
                </td>
                <td>{item.lastError ?? "—"}</td>
              </tr>
            ))}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}


===== apps/web/src/shared/pwa/PwaResourceNotice.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/shared/pwa/PwaResourceNotice.tsx

import { useOnlineStatus } from "./useOnlineStatus";

type PwaResourceNoticeProps = {
  isFromCache?: boolean;
  isRefreshing?: boolean;
  updatedAt?: string | null;
  error?: string | null;
};

export function PwaResourceNotice({
  isFromCache = false,
  isRefreshing = false,
  updatedAt,
  error,
}: PwaResourceNoticeProps) {
  const { isOffline } = useOnlineStatus();

  if (!isOffline && !isFromCache && !isRefreshing && !error) {
    return null;
  }

  return (
    <div className="ck-register-alert">
      {isOffline ? "Você está offline. " : null}
      {isFromCache ? "Exibindo dados salvos localmente. " : null}
      {isRefreshing ? "Atualizando em segundo plano. " : null}
      {updatedAt ? `Última atualização: ${updatedAt}. ` : null}
      {error ? `Aviso: ${error}` : null}
    </div>
  );
}


===== apps/web/src/shared/pwa/PwaScopeProvider.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/shared/pwa/PwaScopeProvider.tsx

import {
  createContext,
  useContext,
  useEffect,
  useMemo,
  type ReactNode,
} from "react";
import { offlineStorage } from "./offlineStorage";
import {
  PWA_ANON_SCOPE,
  serializePwaScope,
  type PwaScope,
} from "./pwaScope";

const PWA_SCOPE_STORAGE_KEY = "corekit:pwa:scope";

type PwaScopeContextValue = {
  scope: PwaScope;
  scopeKey: string;
};

const PwaScopeContext = createContext<PwaScopeContextValue>({
  scope: PWA_ANON_SCOPE,
  scopeKey: serializePwaScope(PWA_ANON_SCOPE),
});

type PwaScopeProviderProps = {
  scope: PwaScope;
  children: ReactNode;
  clearResourceCacheOnScopeChange?: boolean;
};

export function PwaScopeProvider({
  scope,
  children,
  clearResourceCacheOnScopeChange = true,
}: PwaScopeProviderProps) {
  const scopeKey = useMemo(() => serializePwaScope(scope), [scope]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (typeof localStorage === "undefined") return;

    const previousScopeKey = localStorage.getItem(PWA_SCOPE_STORAGE_KEY);

    if (
      clearResourceCacheOnScopeChange &&
      previousScopeKey &&
      previousScopeKey !== scopeKey
    ) {
      offlineStorage.clearCache();
    }

    localStorage.setItem(PWA_SCOPE_STORAGE_KEY, scopeKey);
  }, [clearResourceCacheOnScopeChange, scopeKey]);

  const value = useMemo(
    () => ({
      scope,
      scopeKey,
    }),
    [scope, scopeKey],
  );

  return (
    <PwaScopeContext.Provider value={value}>
      {children}
    </PwaScopeContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function usePwaScope() {
  return useContext(PwaScopeContext);
}


===== apps/web/src/shared/pwa/pwaScope.ts =====

// apps/web/src/shared/pwa/pwaScope.ts

export type PwaScopePerfil = "tenant" | "plataforma" | "anonimo";

export type PwaScope = {
  perfil: PwaScopePerfil;
  locatarioId?: string | null;
  usuarioId?: string | null;
};

export const PWA_ANON_SCOPE: PwaScope = {
  perfil: "anonimo",
  locatarioId: null,
  usuarioId: null,
};

export function serializePwaScope(scope: PwaScope): string {
  return [
    scope.perfil,
    scope.locatarioId ?? "sem-locatario",
    scope.usuarioId ?? "sem-usuario",
  ].join(":");
}

export function buildPwaCacheKey(scope: PwaScope, key: string): string {
  return `${serializePwaScope(scope)}:${key}`;
}
